# 2026-08

## 2026-08-01 — 动作后复核按钮名称并在提交前修正语义

- Transition Observer 沿用现有动作前后两张完整截图和同一次模型调用，输出新增最终 `target`。
  当前名称准确或证据不足时必须原样返回；只有截图直接证明名称错误时才修正。可见文字或符号明确
  的控件不会仅因打开了另一页面而被改成结果页名称。
- Prompt 改为简短的结构化合同：依次声明输入、三项任务、关系标签、证据约束和 JSON 输出。
  字段名只用于准确说明输出职责，移除 `Page / Region identity` 等与本次核验无关的内部说法；
  输出 schema 和判断边界不变。
- Prompt 开头直接说明两张截图、本次可见目标和名称检查任务；移除“只读的视觉核验”“不提出后续
  动作”等 VLM 完成当前任务不需要知道的框架约束。
- 真实 Calendar 保存帧回放显示，仅要求“证据明确时修正”会让模型机械保留
  `Date picker icon (calendar)`。当前合同明确输入 target 只是待校验候选，要求先依据截图独立
  确定控件语义；与控件直接对应的明确可见文字须原样采用，不自行追加控件类型。
- 非 stateful landing 在建立 ActionEdge 前应用该名称：同步 source element、semantic block、
  State/Region 名称账本、replay hint、portable selector 和当前未提交 attempt；原名称保存在
  `evidence.transition_observer.original_target`。已经 committed 的 attempt 和旧边上的其他历史
  attempt 不允许被回写。
- 聚焦离线验证：
  - `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\target-semantics-core-20260801 tests/test_transition_observer.py tests/test_graph_event_ledger.py tests/test_visual_run_state_machine.py tests/test_region_click_ledger.py tests/test_stable_semantic_mapping.py`：`57 passed`；
  - `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\target-semantics-prompts-20260801 tests/test_prompt_catalog.py tests/test_confirmed_traversal_prompt_contracts.py`：`13 passed`；
  - `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\observer-candidate-prompt-20260801 tests/test_transition_observer.py tests/test_prompt_catalog.py tests/test_confirmed_traversal_prompt_contracts.py`：`19 passed`；
  - 目标 Python 文件 `py_compile` 通过。
- 离线用例覆盖 `Date picker -> Manage your calendars` 在 edge commit 前完成替换、旧 attempt 不被
  覆盖、committed attempt 拒绝改写，以及可见 `+` 保持为 `+`。上述离线用例未调用真实 VLM。
- 随后使用历史 `calendar:22`、`calendar:24` 的精确动作前后截图进行了不走缓存的真实
  `qwen3.7-plus` 回放，保持正式 `temperature=0.5`、`top_p=0.9`、thinking 关闭。修订前两组都
  机械保留 `Date picker icon (calendar)`；加入“候选不具有保留优先级”和“明确可见文字原样采用”

  和前后截图保存在 `artifacts/transition_target_semantic_eval_20260801_150032/`。
  这是保存帧上的真实模型证据；未执行 GUI 点击、VM 实时遍历、ActionEdge 运行时改写或完成证书。
- js1 上的单应用 Calendar 受控实时运行验证了运行时改写路径。第一次真实点击原名称为 `+`，动作后

  一个多余 Calendar State。收到 `SIGINT` 后进程以 `130` 退出，本次 Docker 容器正常释放，服务器
  回到运行前的 10 个容器；没有 `completion.json`，也不把这次运行表述为完整遍历或历史错误点验收。

## 2026-08-01 — Page Identity 先关闭应用外弹窗再保存页面

- 两图 Page Identity 在比较页面前先检查当前截图是否有不属于目标应用的临时前景内容。命中时
  输出 `is_interruption=true + same_page=null`，不产生页面结论。
- 登记层复用现有 InterruptionDismisser；Region-lazy 只在这次 Page Identity 明确触发时绕过原有
  启动跳过分支。关闭后用新截图重新运行完整 Page Identity，再进行 State/Region 登记。
- 关闭失败、截图未变化或重新判断仍有 interruption 时以 `UNRESOLVED` 失败关闭，不把当前截图保存为
  Page/State 截图，也不创建新 State。没有增加第二个弹窗 agent，也没有按通知或控件名称硬编码处理。
- 聚焦离线验证：
  - `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\page-identity-interruption-20260801-r3 tests/test_identity_resolver.py tests/test_map_guided_identity.py tests/test_prompt_catalog.py tests/test_region_lazy_runtime.py tests/test_interruption_dismisser_guard.py tests/test_startup_transient_guard.py::test_page_identity_interruption_bypasses_region_lazy_skip`：`89 passed`；
  - 相邻合同命令 `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\page-identity-interruption-adjacent-20260801 tests/test_visual_run_state_machine.py tests/test_visual_router.py tests/test_visual_cache.py tests/test_candidate_identity_and_explorer.py tests/test_confirmed_traversal_prompt_contracts.py`：`136 passed, 20 skipped, 1 failed`。唯一失败是既有 `test_nearest_unexplored_node_batches_frontier_route_planning` 的字符串测试桩不满足当前调度器的元素对象合同，与本次 Page Identity 改动无关，未顺带修改；
  - 目标 Python 文件 `py_compile` 通过。
- 上述均为离线测试；尚未在服务器/VM 中触发真实应用外弹窗并验证关闭、重新截图和后续遍历。

## 2026-08-01 — 仅为空图根页面执行启动弹窗检测

  `_dismiss_interruptions(force_first=True)`。Region-lazy 不再跳过这个空图启动调用。
- 只要图中已有节点，resume 就不重复启动检测；hard reset 也不增加独立检测，继续使用正常
  Page Identity 的 interruption 处理。没有改动 hard reset、Router 或 Grounder/Reviewer Prompt。
- 当前正式 `semantic_inventory` Prompt 已不再输出旧 `is_interruption` 字段；空图启动的
  `force_first` 与 Page Identity 明确触发的清理都直接让现有 Dismisser 有界复查，不恢复旧字段。
- 关闭失败仍以 `startup_interruption_unresolved` 失败关闭，不把受污染画面登记为根节点。
- 聚焦离线验证：

## 2026-08-01 — Click Reviewer 改用点击点居中的干净局部图

- `AnnotationReviewer.review_target()` 不再把红框和黑色十字覆盖图发送给模型。Reviewer 现在同时
  接收未修改的完整截图和一张 201×201 干净局部图；候选点击点固定映射为局部图的唯一中心像素，
  靠近屏幕边缘时用中性灰补齐而不移动中心。红框/十字图继续只作为人工诊断 artifact 保存。
- `TARGET_REVIEW_PROMPT` 相应改为根据完整截图理解页面位置和控件上下文，根据干净局部图判断中心
  像素是否位于目标控件内。Reviewer 的 `accepted=true` 执行门、明确拒绝后的单次重新 grounding、
  fail-closed 行为和点击后的 landing 验证均未改变；Page Identity 与 Transition Observer 未改动。
- 离线测试验证中心像素映射、屏幕边缘补齐、越界点调用模型前拒绝，以及既有 Reviewer 重试与
  artifact 合同：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\review-crop-adjacent-20260801 tests\test_semantic_inventory_grounding.py tests\test_live_targeting.py tests\test_prompt_catalog.py tests\test_confirmed_traversal_prompt_contracts.py`
  -> `82 passed`；目标文件 `py_compile` 通过。
- js1 上使用 Calendar 本轮保存帧和正式 `qwen3.7-plus`、`temperature=0.5`、thinking 关闭做了不执行
  GUI 的真实模型回放。旧 Search 候选点 `[1036,40]` 在移除覆盖标注后被 Reviewer 正确接受；旧 Menu
  候选点 `[1268,39]` 实际位于窗口按钮区域，新 Reviewer 正确拒绝。证据位于
  `/data/shenghonghui/codex_runs/reviewer_crop_probe_20260801_2308/results.json`。
- 同一保存帧继续跑完整 Grounder -> Reviewer 绑定链时，Search 的新 Grounder 回复产生
  `x=1021..1064` 的非法 normalized 坐标，因此在 Reviewer 前失败；Menu 两轮仍把点放在

## 2026-08-01 — Grounder 错误立即反馈并取消 Region 点击边界

- 普通单目标 Grounder 的第一次模型回复只要被格式、坐标、目标确认或前景 surface 检查拒绝，框架就把具体状态、
  原因和已有 `bbox_1000/click_point_1000` 原值写入第二次 `correction_hint`，绕过缓存并改用完整截图。
  第二次仍不合法才失败关闭；传输失败或 agent 不可用没有可反馈的模型回复，继续沿用既有失败路径。
- 任一坐标小于 `0` 或大于 `1000` 现在明确记录为 `coordinate_out_of_range`。该次候选不会进入
  Reviewer，也不会点击；只有纠正后几何合法的候选才交给 Reviewer。
- Region bbox 继续作为第一次定位的近似搜索提示和裁图依据，但不再作为点击合法性边界。删除了
  Grounder 裁图落点越出 Region 后的递归整图重试、LiveTargeting 的 `region_mismatch` 拒绝与执行前
  的重复 Region guard；纠正重试统一使用完整截图，最终是否真是目标由 Reviewer 判断。active surface
  边界仍保留，弹窗目标不能越过其实际前景容器。
- 前景 surface 外的候选现在同样以 `surface_mismatch` 和具体原因反馈 Grounder 纠正一次；这仍保留
  弹窗/菜单不能误点背景的边界。截图或模型传输失败、agent/Reviewer 不可用没有可纠正的 Grounder
  模型结果，不伪装成定位错误反馈。
- 补充 surface 纠正后的聚焦验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\grounder-feedback-surface-20260802 tests/test_semantic_inventory_grounding.py tests/test_live_targeting.py tests/test_traversal_failure_ledger.py`
  -> `77 passed`；目标文件 `py_compile` 与聚焦 `git diff --check` 通过。
- 聚焦离线验证：

  第二轮收到原坐标后才复核，以及 Region 外但 Reviewer 接受的候选正常返回。
- 相邻合同验证：

## 2026-08-02 — Page Identity 只比较两个当前操作界面本身

- 两图 Page Identity 不再复制一份较短的操作界面说明，改为直接使用候选筛选已有的
  `CURRENT_OPERABLE_INTERFACE_DEFINITION_ZH`。没有改变候选筛选、身份解析、缓存或 State 复用流程。
- 裁判明确分别确定两图当前操作界面，只比较这两个界面本身；只有两者相同时才返回
  `same_page=true`。当前操作界面之外共同可见的背景只作上下文，不能单独作为同页依据。保留同一
  页面不同滚动位置仍为同页的现有规则。
- 输出说明改为无单边示例的字段合同，避免唯一 JSON 示例固定展示 `same_page=true`；解析字段没有改变。
- 使用 2026-08-01 Calendar 错误运行的精确两张菜单截图，以正式 `qwen3.7-plus`、
  `temperature=0.5`、thinking 开启、禁用结果缓存做了 3 次独立真实模型调用。三次都返回
  `is_interruption=false + same_page=false`，并分别识别日历管理菜单与应用主菜单。完整 Prompt、
  输入路径、原始错误结果、预期结果和三次原始回复保存在
  `artifacts/page_identity_foreground_fix_20260802/saved_frame_validation.md`。这是保存帧真实模型验证，
  尚不代表重新部署后的实时遍历成功。
- 最小离线检查：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\page-identity-minimal-20260802 tests\test_prompt_catalog.py::test_page_identity_prompts_expose_only_the_visual_judgment tests\test_map_guided_identity.py::test_which_page_shortlists_by_text_then_compares_one_full_screenshot`

## 2026-08-02 — 远端证据拉取禁止递归跟随 OSWorld 链接

- 事故根因确认：本地曾对完整远端运行根执行 `scp -r`；运行根的 `repo/OSWorld` 指向共享
  OSWorld，而共享目录又含 `OSWorld/OSWorld ->` 自身的符号链接环。递归 SCP 解引用链接后持续
  下载 VMware 数据，并在调用超时后仍由底层进程继续传输，最终在本地产生 251.71 GiB 重复目录。
- 新增 `ops/pull_remote_run_evidence.ps1`。入口只打包 `results/` 与根级日志/退出码/起止时间，
  不使用递归 SCP，不包含 `repo/`、`OSWorld/` 或部署压缩包；传输前拒绝结果树符号链接、超过默认
  5 GiB 的未压缩证据/压缩包，以及本地剩余空间不足。下载后还会检查归档路径，再解压并删除临时包。
- `AGENTS.md`、`ops/README.md`、`ops/storage_convention.md`、当前框架入口和环境模块文档同步记录
  “禁止完整运行根 `scp -r`”合同。远端经 owner、链接目标与父目录实路径核对后，仅解除自指的
  `OSWorld/OSWorld` 符号链接；真实 `vmware_vm_data` 保持存在。远端/本地 smoke 临时目录随后删除。
- 验证：PowerShell parser 与 `-PlanOnly` 通过；真实远端正例包含 `repo/OSWorld` 大链接时只拉取
  229-byte 证据包、4 个白名单条目且没有 `repo/`；负例把同类链接放入 `results/` 后在传输前以
  exit 41 拒绝，本地没有残留目录。这是远端文件传输 smoke，不是 GUI、VM、VLM 或遍历验收。

## 2026-08-02 — 独立 Luna observe-assess-act 遍历

- 新增 `--autonomous-agent --autonomous-model` 独立入口，默认使用 `gpt-5.6-luna`。同一个 Luna
  会话直接查看当前完整截图；从第二步起同时查看上一动作前完整截图，在一个回复中判断上一动作的
  实际目标/可见结果并决定下一步。该路径不创建 Region/控件候选，不调用 Page Identity、Grounder、
  Click Reviewer、Transition Observer、Router 或 guided completion。

  坐标范围和模型自己的 `safe|unsafe|uncertain` 标记，执行环境动作并保存 graph/trace/debug 与每个
  attempt 的 before/after 截图。结果语义修正写回同一 attempt；像素变化只作为并列事实。
- 第一次真实 Mingle 运行在 Luna 成功打开 Weekend Plan、准备点击右上菜单后，旧 ArtifactWriter
  的可读多层目录因自然语言目标过长触发 Windows `WinError 206`；运行已关闭环境，证据位于
  `artifacts/luna_autonomous_mingle_20260802_r1/`。自主 attempt 截图随后改为固定短路径
  `action_attempts/<序号>/{before,after}.png`，不改变 trace 中保存的目标语义。
- 修复后的 10-action Local HTML 运行位于
  `artifacts/luna_autonomous_mingle_20260802_r2/20260802/mingle/`：11 次 Luna 观察、5 个截图 State、
  10/10 attempts committed 且 landing-verified、无 pending，以 `max_actions` 正常停止。Luna 自主进入

  平台坐标并直接执行，因此任务内直接动作跳过旧 Grounder 和 Click Reviewer。实际动作账本、fresh

## 2026-08-03 — “探索 X”Agent 使用轻量函数工具和进展型循环退出

- 新增 `runtime/agent_tools.py` 作为目标 Agent 与现有框架能力之间的单一轻量边界。第一版提供
  `recall_page(page_ref)`、`find_route(page_ref)` 和 `inspect_region(region_ref)`：前两者只读压缩页面
  记忆/verified Router，后者复用既有 Region locator + Region-local function-entry 观察。工具只接收
  prompt-local `pN/rN`，稳定 ID、图写入和真实动作仍由框架管理；没有引入 Skill 加载、第二套工具状态
  或新的图 schema。
- Element Agent 的结构化动作新增 `CALL_TOOL`，工具目录、当前 Page 的 Region 目录、最近十二项任务
  事件、累计动作/工具次数以及页面/控件/verified edge/完整滚动 Region 增量一起进入 Prompt。完整任务
  历史仍保留在 runtime ledger；旧事件只从重复 Prompt 中压缩，不丢失审计事实。Agent 每轮输出
  `loop_assessment=not_looping|suspected|confirmed`，确认循环后只能 `DEFER`，或在目标已有验证事实时
  `FINISH`。
- 删除目标任务固定六个实际动作的局部预算。正常任务由目标证据、Agent 明确结束或进展型循环退出；
  全局 traversal `max_actions` 和安全边界不变。框架把连续动作和 `A→B→A→B` 页面轨迹作为客观事实
  交给 Agent；只有同一动作已连续得到明确 `no_effect/no_visible_change` 等无进展结果时才发出紧急循环
  提示，模型收到提示后仍执行同一无进展 GUI 动作，才以 `element_task_loop_unresolved` 暂缓，
  避免无上限截图/调用增长但不按正常有效步数截断任务。
- 目标动作已有 `transitioned_consistent + landing_verified=true` 后，任务进入
  `goal_verified_return_optional`，禁止再次执行目标；Agent 可 `FINISH/DEFER`，也可执行一次直接返回或
  Close。真实落点回到目标来源 Page 后自动完成任务，从而让普通目标探索顺带形成返回动作证据。
- Active element task 到达仍有 pending Region 的页面时，runner 不再先自动消费 Region/scroll 阶段，
  而是继续询问同一 Agent；Agent 可直接依据截图行动或调用 `inspect_region`。没有 active task 时的旧
  Region 初始化和 completion 缺口合同保持不变。
- 将工具边界收拢为声明式 `ToolSpec` 注册表。每项工具声明参数 schema、`EvidenceMode` 和副作用类型，
  Prompt 中的工具目录由注册表生成；dispatcher 只为该次调用准备所需证据。`recall_page/find_route`
  不读取截图，`inspect_region` 使用当前整图，并在 Region bbox 已知时附加即时裁剪图；已预留前后截图
  三种证据模式，但没有增加尚未使用的工具。所有调用统一返回 `ToolResult`，任务历史只保存
  状态、结构化数据及 observation/ledger 变化标记，不保存原始图片。
- 聚焦验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .codex-temp/pytest-agent-tools-final-20260803 tests/test_element_agent.py tests/test_region_lazy_runtime.py tests/test_candidate_identity_and_explorer.py tests/test_confirmed_traversal_prompt_contracts.py`
  -> `61 passed, 20 skipped`；目标 Python `py_compile` 与 `git diff --check` 通过。测试覆盖声明式工具目录、

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous-agent 改为自然语言 Agent-工具协议

- 独立 `--autonomous-agent` 直接采用新协议，不增加第二个开关。持续会话中的同一个 Luna 每轮同时
  判断当前界面是 `new|known|uncertain`，登记自然语言页面名、前景 surface、Region/真实可交互控件，
  复核上一动作，并更新单一 `active_task`、`loop_assessment` 与 `covered/remaining/problems` 后决定
  下一动作。默认 guided semantic traversal 的 Page Identity、Region、Explorer、Router 和 completion
  主链没有改动。
- 新增 `runtime/autonomous_protocol.py` 保存 Agent 编写的自然语言页面地图和页面联系。全局 Prompt 只
  显示自然语言页面名、摘要、surface 与连接，不发送 `pN/rN`、稳定内部 ID、原始 ActionEdge 或全页面
  按钮表；局部 Region/控件只在需要时读取。该地图服务 Agent 记忆，框架仍单独维护内部 State/动作
  证据图，二者不互相冒充。
- Agent 可 `CALL_TOOL show_map/recall_page/find_route/compare_page/inspect_region`。前三者返回自然语言
  地图、指定页局部记忆或建议路线；`compare_page` 为下一轮附上已登记页代表截图，`inspect_region`
  按本轮整图范围生成 Region crop。所有工具只准备记忆、路线或视觉证据，不执行 GUI、不自动导航、
  不替模型判断页面或任务完成；结果仍交给同一 Luna 的下一轮判断。
- 模型仍可直接执行 `CLICK/SCROLL/BACK/WAIT` 或请求 `FINISH`。框架只投递模型标为 `safe` 的 GUI 动作，
  并负责动作前后截图、安全/协议校验、内部图、真实结果账本和全局 action/State 预算。每个实际动作继续
  写入短路径 attempt 证据；新增 `autonomous_natural_map.json`，现有 trace/debug 继续保存结构化登记、
  工具结果与模型后端信息。自主模式仍不生成或宣称 guided `completion.json`。
- 聚焦离线验证：
  `python -m pytest -q tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_fixture_coverage.py`
  -> `22 passed`。该组覆盖协议解析、自然语言地图/五个工具和 fixture 审计合同；它不等于真实模型调用
  工具或完整遍历验收。
- 真实 Luna smoke 共 7 次 Luna 判断、6 个实际 GUI 动作，以人为设置的 `max_actions` 停止；登记 3 个
  自然语言页面、2 条可见变化连接。Luna 从 Chats 进入 Explore，尝试四个 Explore 卡片均无可见变化，
  fixture truth 事后确认它们均为 disabled 或静态 Mini apps，随后转向 Contacts。7/7 保存 rollout 的
  `turn_context.model` 均为 `gpt-5.6-luna`；总计 387850 tokens，其中 cached input 278016。主 Python
  进程约 123 MB，未观察到内存异常。
- 该 smoke 的 `CALL_TOOL=0`：它验证了真实 Luna 的新页面登记、`active_task/loop_assessment`、直接 GUI
  动作、结果复核、自然语言页面连接与 `max_actions` 停止链；五个工具分支仍只有上述离线测试证据。
  本轮不是全遍历完成，也不生成或宣称 guided completion certificate。
- 首次 smoke 后的协议审查补上三项闭环：`uncertain`、不存在的 `known` 和同名 `new` 不再写入正式
  自然地图；`active_task` 跨轮校验要求换目标用 `start`、同目标才能 `continue`，并拒绝
  `complete|blocked|stuck` 同时投递新的 GUI 动作；首次 FINISH 后调用工具仍保持最终巡视状态。动作后
  修正的控件名称也会同步替换自然页面的局部控件记忆。持续 Codex 会话只在首轮重复完整协议，后续发送
  简短续轮要求、最新自然地图和事实账本。
- 修正后的聚焦验证为同一三文件 `26 passed`，另有 canonical-package 架构边界 `1 passed`，目标文件
  `py_compile` 通过。第一次修正后的真实尝试因把固定工具参数信封误改成可选字段，被 Codex CLI 严格
  output-schema 在首轮前拒绝并以 `agent_protocol_error` 停止；没有 GUI 动作，也没有把它计为模型探索
  证据。接口要求对象属性全部列入 `required`，现已恢复固定
  `page_name/region_name/bbox_1000` 信封，并在工具目录中明确未使用字段的空值。
- 最终真实短回归分别以 3/4 个 GUI 动作受控停止。三动作回归登记
  `Chats -> Start a conversation`，验证同一“探索 New chat”任务在抽屉内跨轮 `continue`，并正确记录
  New direct message/New group 的两次无可见变化；4/4 保存 rollout 的实际模型均为
  `gpt-5.6-luna`，共 149131 tokens（cached input 91392）。另一条四动作回归验证搜索开启/关闭、再进入
  Contacts 和顶部加号的任务切换；5/5 turn_context 均为 `gpt-5.6-luna`，共 213434 tokens（cached input
  138240）。两次仍均为 `CALL_TOOL=0`：真实 Luna 在这些清晰截图上没有主动请求工具，因此真实工具回合
  仍未验收，只有 26 项离线 dispatcher/图片回传合同证据；不宣称全遍历完成。

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous Page Identity 提案与主 Agent 复核闭环

- `page_identity` 加入独立 autonomous 工具目录，参数为 `suspected_pages`、`proposed_new_name` 和
  `reason`；框架自动提供当前完整截图、自然地图、候选描述与代表截图。可注入 resolver 的结构化返回
  规范化为 `known|new|uncertain|interruption`，并保留页面名、surface、summary、支持/冲突证据、
  checked candidates 和 reason。未注入 resolver 时明确返回 `uncertain/no model was called`，不以
  fixture 或手写结果冒充真实模型。
- Page Identity 的完整最前景判断、候选排除、interruption、返回和失败规则位于工具 description/内部
  specialist Prompt，不堆入主 Agent 常驻协议。主 Prompt 每轮只给角色、通用审核原则与动态工具目录：
  无 pending 时暴露六个工具，pending 时隐藏 `page_identity`；parser 和 dispatcher 都拒绝未暴露工具。
  reject 后必须经过一个审核回合，下一轮重新暴露 Page Identity 时才把 correction 送给 resolver。
- `known/new` 工具结果只建立绑定当前截图哈希的 pending，不立即写 `autonomous_natural_map.json` 或
  internal graph。下一轮主 Agent 的结构化回复新增 `previous_tool_review`；没有 pending 时为 `null`，
  pending 存在时它是必填，`accept` 后才调用自然地图登记和 State 写入，`reject` 清除提案并把
  reason/correction 传给下次
  `page_identity`，`uncertain` 保留 unresolved pending。复核前截图变化会以 `stale_screenshot` 作废，
  unresolved 期间只允许继续调用工具或 WAIT，不投递 GUI 动作。没有增加单独 accept 工具。
- 为保持 Codex CLI 严格 output-schema 可接受，`tool_arguments` 的六个属性全部列入 `required`；既有五个
  工具把新增三项填为 `[]/""/""`，`page_identity` 把旧三项填为 `""/""/null`。这是结构化信封扩展，
  不改变原五个 dispatcher 的实际行为。
- 保留当前 Codex CLI 持续主 Agent transport、原五个工具和既有直接动作合同；没有迁移 Responses API，
  没有新增 API key 要求，也没有改 guided Page Identity/Region/Grounder/Reviewer/Router。默认 CLI 入口
  尚未装配专用真实 identity resolver，因此本次只验证可注入闭环，不宣称真实 Luna Page Identity 已运行。
- Tier 3 聚焦离线验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp=.codex-temp/pytest-page-identity-final2 tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_fixture_coverage.py tests/test_architecture_boundaries.py::test_visual_traversal_canonical_packages_and_compatibility_shims`
  -> `33 passed`。目标四文件 `py_compile` 通过；tracked 文档和四个未跟踪目标文件的 `git diff --check`
  均无空白错误，只有现有 LF→CRLF 提示。测试覆盖 resolver 自动证据、真实 resolver 缺席时
  fail-closed、accept/reject/uncertain、
  rejection correction 回传、截图 stale 和 NEW 提交时机；未运行真实付费模型、VM/device、长遍历或
  completion certificate。

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous 11 工具协议、轻量框架校验与真实 Luna 闭环

- 自主协议收敛为 11 个公开工具：
  `page_identity/route_to/report_record_error/finish_exploration`、
  `map_regions/inspect_region/reconcile_regions`、`click/scroll/navigate/restore_state`。删除旧公开
  `show_map/recall_page/find_route/compare_page`、旧 crop-only `inspect_region` 和直接
  `CLICK/SCROLL/BACK/WAIT/FINISH`；严格 output schema 每轮只包含当前状态可调用的工具。
- 主 Agent 负责页面、Region、按钮、动作结果和工具返回是否语义合理；框架只检查 JSON 结构、必需
  定位键、本轮工具暴露、latest frame、坐标、RegionScan/bbox、pending transaction 与真实执行事实。
  删除 `active_task` 目标字符串一致性、解释文字非空、reject correction 非空等门槛；动作后误填
  `not_applicable` 时降为 `uncertain`，不让整轮失效。Page Identity 只要求 known 能映射已登记页面、
  new 能取得唯一自然页面名等落账完整性。
- Page/Region 工具结果绑定当前 frame，先由同一主 Agent 用 `previous_tool_review` 接受、拒绝或保持
  uncertain。Region 工具保留 accepted proposal、current-frame bbox 与可暂停 RegionScan；entry 账本
  区分 discovered/attempted/verified/unresolved，模型观察不能直接晋升 verified。普通 click 使用主 Agent
  整图坐标，独立 VLM Click Reviewer 只审核红点与安全性，不重定位；scroll 必须绑定 active scan 和
  当前 Region bbox；可逆 click 由 temporary-state/restore_state 闭环。
- 第一次真实 Mingle 尝试中，主 Agent 自己发现并通过 `report_record_error` 上报：空自然地图首屏调用
  Page Identity 时，specialist Prompt 的“候选缺失返回 uncertain”使 Chats 根页永远无法登记。随后把
  工具规则改为 `registered_page_count=0` 时可按清晰视觉证据返回 `new`；地图非空而 Agent 提供无效
  suspected name 时，框架改为提供全部已登记自然页面候选。单独用真实 Chats 截图调用 specialist 后，
  Luna 返回 `status=new, page_name=Chats`，明确引用根页初始化规则。
- 第二次真实尝试暴露落账关联问题：点击进入新页面后，主 Agent 第一轮已正确返回 `changed`，但页面
  pending 审核完成时框架误用后续非 GUI 工具轮的 `previous_action` 覆盖它。`PendingAction` 现在冻结紧邻
  GUI 动作的第一份 VLM 评估，待落点页面登记后写回同一 attempt；后续 Page/Region/route/review 轮不再
  覆盖。达到 `max_actions` 只停止新 GUI 动作，不再截断最后一次动作的 Page Identity、评估与边提交。
- Tier 3 聚焦验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_entry_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py --basetemp .codex-temp/all-autonomous-tools-20260803-e`
  -> `81 passed in 3.00s`。覆盖 11 个工具 schema/目录、Page/Region review、root identity、action/result、
  entry/scan/restore、Click Reviewer reject、最后动作待页面身份时的延迟结算和语义文字空值容忍。
- 最新真实 Local HTML Mingle 短跑：
  `python gui_rewalk/run_visual_traversal.py --vm_provider local_html --app_name mingle --clean_start --autonomous-agent --autonomous-model gpt-5.6-luna --max_states 20 --max_actions 3 --result_dir artifacts/autonomous_mingle_smoke_20260803_e --autonomous_fixture_audit --no_live_monitor`
  正常以人为设置的 `max_actions` 停止。主 Agent 登记 Chats 根页，打开并主动关闭搜索状态，调用并接受
  `map_regions`，再进入并登记 Weekend Plan 聊天页面；3 个点击均为 `observed_change`，3 次独立 Reviewer
  均为 `approve`。自然地图形成 `Chats 根页面 --Weekend Plan 会话--> Weekend Plan 聊天页面`，最后一次
  点击在动作上限后仍完成身份审核和边提交，没有 `awaiting_observation`。8 个主/专用 rollout 的实际
  `turn_context.model` 全部为 `gpt-5.6-luna`。该三动作 Codex CLI 实验共 15 个模型回合，rollout 计数为
  957350 total tokens：945958 input、451840 cached input、11392 output；大头来自 Codex 基础/工作区上下文
  和持续主会话历史，不代表精简 Responses API 的预期成本。主 Python 进程保持百 MB 级，未复现 18 GB
  异常。
- 该运行只是真实 VLM + Local HTML 的三动作协议/恢复/落账 smoke，不是 Android、任意应用全遍历、

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous 默认切换到真实 Qwen API

- `--autonomous-agent` 默认后端改为 `qwen_api`，默认模型改为 `qwen3.7-plus`；复用仓库现有
  `GUIGenAgent` 和 `DASHSCOPE_API_KEY`，主 Agent、Page/Region specialist 与 Click Reviewer 均直接走
  DashScope-compatible API。密钥只从环境读取，不写源码或 artifact。`codex_cli` 只保留为显式调试后端。
- Qwen adapter 在每轮 prompt 附加当前动态工具 JSON Schema，使用既有多图 transport/JSON parser，保存
  role、实际 model、prompt/completion token 与 transport attempts；回复仍由现有结构与可执行事实校验，
  不恢复页面/按钮语义字符串门槛。
- 真实 Local HTML Mingle 三动作运行：
  `python gui_rewalk/run_visual_traversal.py --vm_provider local_html --app_name mingle --clean_start --autonomous-agent --autonomous-backend qwen_api --autonomous-model qwen3.7-plus --max_states 20 --max_actions 3 --result_dir artifacts/autonomous_mingle_qwen_api_smoke_20260803 --autonomous_fixture_audit --no_live_monitor`
  正常以 `max_actions` 停止并释放环境。Qwen 登记 Chats 与 Contacts，形成
  `Chats --Contacts tab--> Contacts`；第一次点击 Contacts 成功变化。主 Agent 随后给出的 New friends 坐标
  位于设备外，独立 Qwen Click Reviewer 拒绝且没有执行；主 Agent读取理由后调用并接受 `map_regions`，
  用修正坐标重试。fixture 显示 New friends 入口 disabled，Groups 也无页面变化，二者均如实落为
  `no_visible_change`，没有伪登记新节点。最后无 pending action。
- 该运行实际 15 次 Qwen API transport request，全部一次成功：106459 prompt tokens、9513 completion
  tokens、115972 total tokens。相同三动作的 Codex CLI/Luna 协议 smoke 为 957350 total tokens，因此此
  transport 样本少 841378 tokens（约 87.9%）；应用路线不同，数字只说明 transport 上下文开销，不是模型
  能力 A/B。
- Tier 3 聚焦验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_entry_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py --basetemp .codex-temp/all-autonomous-qwen-20260803-final`
  -> `82 passed in 4.22s`；并对五个 autonomous runtime 模块与 `run_visual_traversal.py` 执行
  `python -m py_compile`，通过。新增覆盖真实 transport 形状的假 Qwen 主回合与 specialist 回合，不把离线
  fake 结果表述为真实 API 证据；真实 API 证据来自上述 Mingle 运行。

## 2026-08-03 — 共享 Android 服务器禁用 ADB daemon 重启

- deploy-asr 的隔离 Android Clock 启动预检发现：AndroidWorld controller 固定通过 host ADB daemon
  5037 连接指定 emulator serial；仅让模拟器注册到另一个 daemon 端口会在 GUI/Qwen 运行前以
  `device emulator-5672 not found` 失败。该失败没有进入应用或执行 GUI 动作。
- `AndroidGUIGenEnv._restart_adb_server()` 新增 opt-in 保护
  `GUI_REWALK_ALLOW_ADB_RESTART=0`。共享服务器 runner 可预先把自己的唯一 serial 注册到现有 5037，
  同时禁止环境在启动或截图恢复时执行全局 `adb kill-server/start-server`；默认值仍为允许，不改变本地
  独占 ADB 的既有恢复行为。
- 第二次启动已完成 emulator、AndroidWorld controller 和 Clock data-preserving launch，但首个 Qwen 请求
  在发送前失败。根因是 Windows 标准输入向 SSH 传递环境值时保留了尾部 `\r`，使 Authorization header
  非法；诊断异常意外包含原始 header，因此该 key 必须轮换，且本轮在用户完成轮换前停止，不继续调用。
  `GUIGenAgent` 现在统一对 `DASHSCOPE_API_KEY` 执行 `strip()` 后再构造客户端，避免 shell 换行进入 header；
  key 仍只从环境读取，不写入源码或 artifact。
- Tier 2 聚焦验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_android_env_log_guard.py tests/test_vlm_timeout.py --basetemp .codex-temp/android-qwen-secret-guard-20260803`
  -> `10 passed in 2.81s`；`python -m py_compile gui_rewalk/env/android_gui_gen_env.py gui_rewalk/env/gui_gen_agent.py`
  通过。覆盖共享 host 禁止 ADB restart，以及带首尾空白/CRLF 的 key 在构造客户端前被清理；均为离线
  合同验证，不替代换 key 后的真实 Clock/Qwen 重跑。
- 后续真实短跑还发现 PowerShell 管道可能在 key 最前面附加 Unicode BOM（`U+FEFF`）；普通 `strip()`
  不会移除该字符，OpenAI client 因 Authorization header 不能 ASCII 编码而在请求前失败。构造 client 前
  现在同时去除首部 BOM 和外围空白，原环境变量仍不记录到源码、日志或 artifact；对应构造测试覆盖
  `BOM + 空格 + CRLF` 组合。最终与拒绝反馈一起运行的聚焦测试见下条。

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous 拒绝反馈可由主 Agent 直接纠正

- 框架拒绝现在除原 `detail` 外统一附带结构化 `rejection`：`reason_code/reason` 说明事实原因，
  `suggested_next_tool` 给出可选纠正工具，`retry_after` 给出允许重试条件，`correction` 给出纠正方向。
  最近记录摘要把该对象原样交回主 Agent。覆盖页面身份未解决、工具参数或当前目录不合法、完成证据缺口、
  模型未标 safe、Click Reviewer 拒绝和动作转换失败；框架没有新增页面/按钮语义字符串判定。
- 该反馈不改变动态工具目录或 Page/Region 调度；下一步仍由主 Agent 决定。页面身份未解决时会建议
  `page_identity`，Click Reviewer 拒绝时会建议根据其视觉理由修正目标或点位后重试 `click`，但框架不
  强制模型采用该建议。
- Tier 3 聚焦离线验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py tests/test_vlm_timeout.py tests/test_android_env_log_guard.py --basetemp .codex-temp/pytest-rejection-reason-only-20260803`
  -> `36 passed in 5.76s`；`autonomous_loop.py`、`autonomous_protocol.py` 与 `gui_gen_agent.py` 的
  `py_compile` 通过。测试证明
  rejection 进入真实主 Prompt、接受 Page Identity 后原动作仍能正常提交，以及 Click Reviewer 的拒绝理由
  和纠正建议可见。尚未在本条记录中宣称真实模型运行结果。
- 后续 Android Clock 短跑中，Bedtime 页面在 Page Identity specialist 与主 Agent 复核之间发生被动像素
  更新，旧 exact-frame 门连续三次把 `new` 提案判为 `stale_screenshot`，虽然期间没有执行任何 GUI 动作。
  Page Identity pending 现在保留到主 Agent 在最新截图上明确 accept/reject/uncertain；动态内容不再由框架
  直接否决身份提案。Region bbox/scan 的 frame 失效规则不变。按本轮“跳过不必要测试”的要求，仅运行
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_autonomous_loop.py::test_pending_identity_can_be_reviewed_after_passive_frame_change --basetemp .codex-temp/pytest-passive-identity-20260803`
  -> `1 passed in 0.90s`，并对 `autonomous_loop.py` 执行 `py_compile` 通过；未运行其他回归。

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous 落地页身份先于 Region 工具

- Android Clock 监视运行暴露出真实顺序缺口：从 Clock 点击进入尚未登记的 Alarm 后，普通点击因
  `page_identity_unresolved` 被正确拒绝，但动态目录仍暴露 `map_regions`。Agent 随后接受了 Alarm 的
  Region 提案；由于 Alarm 尚未进入自然语言页面地图，该结果只存在于独立 `alarm` Region 账本，无法
  绑定到正式页面记录。
- 动态工具目录现在在真实 GUI 动作的落地登记仍 pending 时隐藏

  输入需要退格，菜单需要点外部或 Back，对话框需要 Cancel。旧工具在 Timer 已由普通退格清空后又点击
  原 `1` 键，重新输入 1 秒，并仅因像素变化便把账本误标为 `restored`；主 Agent 随后根据最新截图发现
  仍显示 `00:00:01`，自行再次点击退格纠正了真实界面。

  `needs_restore` 账目。主 Agent 使用普通 `click/navigate` 自行选择恢复动作，并在下一轮
  `previous_action.temporary_state` 报告 `not_applicable|restored|still_changed|uncertain` 以及对应

  更新账本；不匹配报告作为可纠正反馈保存，不否决同轮

## 2026-08-03 — autonomous Clock 实跑后的复核衔接与状态去重

- 真实 Android Clock/Qwen 运行在第二次到达 Settings 时，由 Page Identity 提出 `known`；待主 Agent 复核的动态目录却把复核通过后所需的 `map_regions` 一并隐藏，模型连续选择未暴露工具并以 `agent_protocol_error` 结束。现在待 Page Identity 复核期间只隐藏重复的 `page_identity`，保留 `map_regions`；主 Agent 的 accept 会在同轮工具分发前写入页面归属。
- `click` 的状态字段说明收窄为设置、输入、选择或运行过程，明确页面跳转、菜单、对话框和展开内容不需要恢复。动作落地轮的状态报告优先复用该动作创建的 temporary-state 键，避免“Top-right menu button”与“Overflow Menu”等别名生成两份账目。
- 主 Prompt 除当前页代表去重外，也要求依据全局 connections 跳过已经被真实边证明通向同一已知页面或相同浅层功能的跨页重复入口。
- Clock 重试中 Style 已从 Digital 改为 Analog 并真实恢复，但动作目标分别叫 Analog/Digital，旧账本把它们当成两条状态。恢复动作的 before/current 现在可与同页未恢复账目反向匹配，合并回原状态轴；response schema 进一步强调 control 应写 Style 等稳定状态轴，菜单/对话框开关不属于临时状态。
- 同一运行里 Snooze length 与 Silence after 已分别 verified，模型仍在短期记录和入口账本都可见时重复打开，并继续报告 progress。`click` 因此增加用途枚举 `explore|navigate|restore|dismiss`；只有同页入口已 verified 且最近刚有同目标真实结果时才拒绝重复 `explore`，并返回尝试次数、最近结果和改用未探索目标的建议。导航重放、状态恢复和关闭不受此提醒影响。
- 第二次 Clock 重试中，重复提醒真实拦下了 Alarm 页 Overflow Menu 的近邻重复 `explore`，Qwen收到尝试次数和最近结果后没有原样重试，改为 `inspect_region`。该 Region 结果又暴露两个账本问题：模板把 8:30/9:00 写进参数而未折叠，且已登记 `button` 在直接点击时因默认 `control` 被另建同名 entry。specialist 现要求 `function_template` 不含具体数据；直接点击优先绑定同页同 Region 同名现有 entry，同模板合并时保留首个代表。
- 聚焦验证：首轮 Page/Region/状态键检查 `3 passed`；追加状态反向合并与近邻重复提醒后运行 `python -m pytest --basetemp <project-temp> -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py::test_catalog_is_compact_dynamic_and_grants_only_explicit_authority tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py::test_click_accepts_full_screen_boundaries_on_latest_frame tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_recent_verified_repeat_only_blocks_exploration_intent tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_restored_report_matches_the_open_inverse_state_axis -q` → `4 passed`；目标 `py_compile` 通过。
- 真实 Android Clock/Qwen 重试共执行 23 个 GUI 动作、57 次后端调用，登记 Clock、Settings、外部系统 Date & time 及三条真实导航关系。它验证了页面身份拒绝反馈、自我纠正、待 Page Identity 复核后直接 `map_regions`、菜单不进入 temporary-state、开关改动恢复和系统页返回；最终因确认出现已验证入口重复循环而由测试人员精确终止，`run.exit_code=143`，不是完成证书。证据保存在 `artifacts/android_clock_qwen_agent_final_20260803_230455/`。
- 追加 `click.intent` 后的第二次真实 Clock/Qwen 重试执行 7 个 GUI 动作，验证了真实重复拒绝与 Agent 改选工具；随后确认 8:30/9:00 两个等价 Expand 仍因模板/entry 身份问题被分别执行，因而精确终止，`run.exit_code=143`，不是完成证书。证据保存在 `artifacts/android_clock_qwen_agent_final_20260803_232110/`。
- 入口复用与模板代表的聚焦验证：`python -m pytest --basetemp <project-temp> -p no:cacheprovider tests/test_autonomous_entry_tools.py::test_direct_action_reuses_same_named_inspected_entry_across_control_type tests/test_autonomous_entry_tools.py::test_repeated_dynamic_items_fold_into_one_function_template tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py::test_dynamic_tool_definitions_are_analysis_only_without_hidden_scroll tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_click_tool_uses_one_execution_path_and_records_entry_result -q` → `4 passed`；目标 `py_compile` 通过。
- 使用第二次真实运行保存的 Alarm 完整帧 `e877bd9080a18125...` 对修改后的 Qwen Region specialist 做单次真实复放；8:30/9:00 两张卡分别返回相同的 `select_alarm_card`、`toggle_alarm`、`expand_alarm_card`，不再把时间或参数写进模板。该结果验证 Prompt 输出，不是新的 GUI 遍历或完成验收。

## 2026-08-04 — autonomous 由 Agent 声明同质、框架派发入口任务并支持续跑

- 撤销上一版按 `function_template`、同名目标或 control 类型自动折叠入口的做法。Region specialist 现在只能
  通过同帧 `equivalent_occurrences`，或跨观察显式
  `equivalent_to_entry_id + equivalence_reason` 报告同质关系；已知落点可附
  `equivalent_destination_page`。框架不做任何字符串相似度判断，新入口没有显式 ID 关系就单独登记。
- 框架从 Agent 登记的 `discovered|unresolved` 代表入口中一次派发一个 `exploration_task`，包含入口 ID、所属
  Page/Region、自然语言目标和当前已知路线。主 Agent 只报告 `task_progress`，仍可自行处理导航、回溯、弹窗、
  恢复和顺路发现；任务入口 verified 或显式关联为 inferred 后才派发下一项。`click` 删除 `intent`，改为可选
  `entry_id`：只有直接尝试已登记入口时绑定，其他中间动作留空，框架不因 target 同名自动绑定。
- Agent 显式声明的等价 occurrence 在代表入口真实验证后可写入推断边。自然地图把它标为
  `provenance=agent_inferred_equivalence`；内部 ActionEdge 使用 `action_type=INFERRED`、
  `committed=false`、`landing_verified=false` 和 `no_gui_action=true`，因此“探索一个等于探索一组”不会伪造成
  每个入口都执行过。以后真实点击仍可另外写入 observed 边。
- `--autonomous-agent --resume <graph.json>` 现在恢复同一目录里的内部 graph、自然地图、Region/scan、entry、
  temporary-state 和最近 8 条 trace，再增量继续。旧 frame bbox、待审核 Region proposal 和旧 current page
  不复用；当前页由新截图重新报告。历史 Action ID 会推进新计数，中断时仍 pending 的入口重新开放为任务，
  但旧 direct-action 回填的泛化目标不会仅因 unresolved 被自动调度。
- completion 与任务调度使用同一入口边界：仅 Agent 通过 Region Inspection 登记或由中断任务重新开放的

## 2026-08-04 — autonomous 入口结算要求真实落点满足任务，并切断连续争议上报

- Android Clock Alarm 实跑连续暴露同一结算缺口。`ae120 / Snooze/Add Button` 的实际点击命中了
  `Pause Alarm Button` 并再次打开日期选择器；随后 `ae122 / Vibrate Toggle` 的实际结果是折叠整张闹钟卡，
  而非切换振动。两次主 Agent 都在 `target_name/summary` 中正确描述了真实落点或异常结果，但旧 runtime
  只要结果为 `changed|no_visible_change` 就把绑定入口结为 verified，因而错误推进到后续任务。
- 主响应新增严格布尔字段 `previous_action.matches_intent`，由主 Agent 根据动作前后图判断真实落点与结果是否
  满足 pending 动作/绑定 entry。框架不做按钮名字符串比较；仅当该字段为 true 且 outcome 已可靠观察时才
  verified。false 时仍保存真实动作边、修正后的实际目标与前后帧，但原 entry 保持 unresolved，并返回结构化
  `action_did_not_match_entry`，提示 Agent 复查 Region/入口关系或在真实目标可见时重试。
- 同一实跑随后进入无 GUI 进展循环：Agent 因把已覆盖 Expand Arrow 继续携带 `entry_id` 而被正确拒绝，虽然
  拒绝明确提示“必要导航请留空 entry_id”，仍连续四轮调用 `report_record_error`。该工具只记录争议，重复调用
  不会改变截图或任务。动态目录现在在一次上报后的紧邻轮暂不暴露它，迫使 Agent 从其他合法工具或 GUI 动作
  中继续选择；没有新增局部步数上限，也没有替模型硬编码导航目标。
- 聚焦离线验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\m033 tests\test_autonomous_loop.py::test_parse_turn_accepts_tool_call_envelope tests\test_autonomous_loop.py::test_no_visible_change_verifies_bound_entry_and_closes_task tests\test_autonomous_loop.py::test_changed_action_on_another_control_keeps_bound_entry_unresolved tests\test_autonomous_loop.py::test_record_error_is_not_exposed_on_the_immediately_following_turn`
  -> `4 passed in 1.10s`；`autonomous_loop.py` 通过 `py_compile`。这是离线合同验证，部署后的 Android 续跑
  确认尚待完成。Snooze/Pause 对照、Vibrate 折叠前后图，以及修复前 entries/trace/debug 已保存到持续监控
  证据目录；历史错误账本没有被覆盖。

## 2026-08-04 — Click Reviewer 独立核对目标语义，并禁止猜测破坏性动作的后续确认

- 部署 `previous_action.matches_intent` 后的 Android 续跑证明，结算槽本身仍不足以纠正被任务名锚定的同一
  主 Agent。它在清楚看到 `Pause alarm` 行和日期选择器后，仍把该加号称为 `Snooze/Add Button`，并返回
  `matches_intent=true`；旧 Reviewer 的 `observed_target` 已准确写成“Pause alarm 行右侧的 plus icon”，却只因
  点位可交互且安全而 approve，没有判断该视觉控件是否真的符合请求 target。
- Click Reviewer schema 新增必填布尔 `target_matches_request`。该值仍由独立 VLM 根据当前截图的可见标签、
  行/分组和可靠视觉证据判断，runtime 只做结构化门控；`approve + point_matches_target=true + risk=safe` 之外还
  必须满足它为 true。Prompt 明确禁止用主 Agent 的 purpose/expected_result 给控件反向编造含义，并用本次
  `Snooze/Add` 对 `Pause alarm` 行加号作为错误示例。没有增加按钮名字符串比较。
- 同一续跑随后派发 `ae123 / Delete Button`。主 Agent 说“点击后应该出现确认框”，Reviewer 也凭 Android Clock
  惯例猜测会有 confirmation/snackbar，遂以 safe 放行；真实点击立即删除了 8:30 闹钟，没有确认步骤。监控在
  动作落地后、框架读取 after frame 前冻结进程，并通过 ADB 只读抓图确认页面只剩 9:00 闹钟。Prompt 现要求：
  不得把可能存在的后续确认、Snackbar 或 Undo 当成本次点击安全证据；当前视觉证据不能排除本次点击直接
  删除、提交或产生外部影响时必须 fail closed。
- 聚焦离线验证：
  `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\t\m036 tests\test_autonomous_action_tools.py::test_click_reviewer_rejects_read_only_regions_even_when_safe tests\test_autonomous_loop.py::test_click_reviewer_receives_discovery_only_scope_and_expected_result tests\test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_independent_click_review_rejects_without_dispatch tests\test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_click_review_rejects_interactive_point_on_a_different_target`
  -> `4 passed in 1.20s`；`autonomous_action_tools.py` 与 `autonomous_loop.py` 通过 `py_compile`。真实删除前后图、
  Reviewer 原文、executed/uncommitted graph、trace、entry 和完整 debug 已保存；修复后尚待 Android 续跑确认。

### 2026-08-04 - Unsafe entry rejection now closes task dispatch without claiming verification

- Live Android Clock monitoring confirmed that the independent click reviewer now rejects the visible Delete button before GUI dispatch. The previous unsafe run and the new rejection are preserved separately under `artifacts/android_clock_qwen_agent_final_20260803_230455/resume_monitor_20260804_evidence/M037-*`.
- A follow-on defect kept `ae123 / Delete Button` task-eligible after that rejection, so the framework repeatedly dispatched the same impossible task and the Agent alternated record-error reports with invalid turns. An independently rejected click now removes the exact bound entry from task scheduling only when the reviewer both confirms `target_matches_request=true` and returns `risk=unsafe`. The entry remains `unresolved`, retains the reviewer reason, and is never represented as verified.
- Click review requests now include the bound ledger entry's Page, Region, target, control type, and current value. This lets the reviewer distinguish a similarly named or shaped control in another Region without framework string matching.
- Verification: `python -m py_compile gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_loop.py gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_entry_tools.py gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_action_tools.py` passed. Focused tests for unsafe ledger disposition, task rescheduling, reviewer request context, and reviewer prompt contract passed: `4 passed in 0.86s`. Live confirmation of the new task-disposition behavior is pending; this is not a traversal completion claim.
- The resumed experimental ledger contained an unsafe rejection written by the older binary, so it required a one-record evidence-backed repair rather than a general migration layer. After the task became null, the Agent still repeated the old dispute because the catalog hid `report_record_error` for only one immediate turn. Suppression now survives intervening invalid turns and observations until a real GUI action or another tool creates progress. Focused verification: `2 passed in 1.02s`; `autonomous_loop.py` compiled. Live confirmation is pending.
- Live confirmation passed: the next Agent turn had no Delete task, stopped reporting the dispute, navigated Clock -> Settings, scrolled to the requested bottom Alarms Region, and completed that Region. Monitoring then found that it also reinspected two already-complete visible Regions, creating five redundant inferred occurrence entries. `inspect_region` now rejects a Region whose framework coverage is already complete and points the Agent to an incomplete Region or `reconcile_regions` for a real visible structural change. Verification: `3 passed in 1.26s`; `autonomous_loop.py` compiled. Live confirmation of this latest guard is pending.

### 2026-08-04 - M043 exclude operating-system input surfaces from application traversal

- Live Clock traversal on Search City accepted `Virtual Keyboard` as a Region and registered ae148-ae161 for the Android IME Search, letter group, Backspace, Settings, Clipboard, Voice, and related keys. The scheduler immediately dispatched ae148 and action 191 clicked the keyboard Search key; the application image did not visibly change. This was a VLM ownership error, not a JSON/geometry error.
- `map_regions`, `inspect_region`, their dynamic tool descriptions, and the main-Agent review contract now say that software keyboards/IME and operating-system chrome are environment state rather than target-application Regions or functions. An entirely external input surface produces `entries=[]`; a proposal that registers such controls must be rejected by the main Agent. No framework string blacklist or semantic merge was added.

Explicit jq repair scripts and post-repair snapshots preserve how ae148-ae161 and the mistaken Region were excluded before resuming. Post-fix Android confirmation remains pending.

### 2026-08-04 - M044 bind the fresh resume page before exposing Region tools

- The first fresh screenshot after resume was correctly reported as known `Clock`, but the dynamic catalog had been computed while `current_page` was intentionally unbound. It therefore exposed `map_regions`; the Agent called it and accepted a duplicate four-Region proposal. The persisted Region ledger preserved its prior complete coverage, so this incident wasted model calls without corrupting the saved completion flags.
- When a resumed natural map already has registered pages but no fresh current-page binding, the catalog now omits all Region tools for that one turn. After the current screenshot is reported known/new, the next turn uses the bound page's real Region state. A pending known Page Identity result likewise uses the existing candidate page state. A structural `regions_already_mapped` rejection prevents stale/illegal `map_regions` calls from reaching the specialist.
- The existing catalog test was corrected to assert that ordinary `scroll` remains exposed, matching the previously approved full action-space contract.
- Focused verification: `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp=.codex-temp/pytest-m044-final-0639 tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_dynamic_catalog_combines_page_region_and_action_without_empty_fallback tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_resumed_unbound_page_does_not_offer_first_page_region_mapping tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_pending_page_review_exposes_post_accept_region_mapping tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py::test_map_regions_is_rejected_before_specialist_for_mapped_page` -> `4 passed in 0.64s`; `autonomous_loop.py` compiled.
- Evidence is saved as `M044-known-clock-was-offered-map-regions-*`, `M044-known-clock-existing-regions-but-map-offered.png`, and `M044-resume-0641-{run,wrapper}.log`. Live post-fix confirmation remains pending.

### 2026-08-04 - M045 stop repeated successful route queries before they become a no-action loop

- The continued Clock run completed the Stopwatch Regions, then needed to navigate to Bedtime. Its first click correctly failed closed because it bound the already inferred occurrence `ae179`; the rejection explicitly said that navigation must leave `entry_id` empty. `route_to(Stopwatch, Bedtime)` then returned the same direct Bedtime-tab step three consecutive times on the unchanged Stopwatch screenshot, while the Agent incorrectly reported each query as progress and never executed the route.

After any route query, `route_to` is omitted from the dynamic catalog across passive observations and invalid turns until a real GUI result or another tool creates a new substantive boundary. This prevents identical read-only route loops without selecting the target, route, point, or next tool for the Agent.

### 2026-08-04 - Replace incident-specific autonomous Prompt patches with general contracts

- Audited the M043-M060 fixes after the user rejected application-, control-, and one-incident-specific Prompt rules. The autonomous Region inventory Prompt was not the previously validated guided Region inventory Prompt and had a wider entry boundary, so it could promote operation-screen controls such as a schedule value into traversal entries.
- The autonomous Region inventory now uses the general guided boundary: an entry must enter or switch independent function content, expand hidden actionable function content, or remove a restriction; controls that only complete the current operation, change a value, toggle state, are disabled, or are confirmed not to expose new function content are not traversal entries. The Prompt contains no Clock-, alarm-, keyboard-, picker-, or weekday-specific examples.
- The main Agent now always receives the complete public tool catalog. Tool visibility no longer encodes call order or current validity; a state-invalid call is rejected with factual state, correction, and retry information. Region specialist proposals remain pending for main-Agent review, and an accepted review may be followed by the Agent's next chosen tool in the same turn instead of a forced wait.
- Replaced accumulated live-incident prose with general contracts for current-task focus, necessary navigation and recovery, first-seen intermediate Page/edge registration, before/after evidence, state restoration, safety, and loop awareness. Unrelated known functions, controls, and Regions are deferred to later framework dispatch.

### 2026-08-04 - Schedule page survey and entry probing as two persistent Agent tasks

- Replaced the autonomous loop's entry-only/nullable task contract with exactly two persistent task types. `survey_page` establishes or extends a Page's identity, Regions, scroll coverage and distinct entries; `explore_entry` routes to one registered entry, performs the real probe and records/restores its outcome. A newly registered or incomplete Page survey is scheduled before entry probing, while an intermediate Page seen during entry routing may be minimally registered without replacing that entry task. When both queues are complete, the task remains `survey_page` with `phase=completion_ready` instead of becoming null.
- Added optional main-Agent `page_update` output for simple latest-frame Region and entry deltas. The Agent may pair it with a tool call or set `action=null` for an observation-only/update-only turn. Runtime validation is structural and factual only: the update must belong to the latest registered Page/frame, entries must belong to accepted or same-update Regions, and referenced entry/equivalence destinations must exist. Accepted updates reuse the existing Region and entry ledgers with `main_agent_page_update` provenance; complex, uncertain and long-scroll collection continues through the optional Region specialists.
- Completion now reports every registered Page without any accepted Region as a factual survey gap. Agent-declared `coverage_complete` can close a simple Region directly; `survey_needed` keeps a follow-up page survey scheduled. Directly reported entries become normal task candidates, and the framework still never infers functional equivalence from names or strings.
- Removed the old hidden behavior that discarded the next action chosen on an accepted Region-review turn. Review is applied first and the same response's selected tool/action is then handled normally; `action=null` is the explicit way to make that turn review-only.

No Android/Qwen live traversal was run for this change.

### 2026-08-04 - Keep the main response lean and let the Agent close page surveys

- The state-specific main-Agent schema now always requires only `screen` and nullable `action`. It adds `previous_action` only while a real GUI action awaits settlement and `previous_tool_review` only while a specialist proposal awaits review. `page_update` and loop assessment remain optional, and `task_progress` is removed from the live response schema. The parser still accepts older verbose responses and fills omitted optional records with neutral defaults.
- `page_update` now requires only the owning Page name; Region deltas require only a Region name and entry deltas only their Region plus target. Summary, controls, scroll flag, coverage, equivalence and current-frame bbox are optional. A bbox remains transient frame-local evidence and is not stable Page memory.
- The main Agent may close a page survey by reporting every Region it identified as `coverage_complete=true` with `survey_needed=false`. A scrollable Region may be completed from the Agent's own consecutive scroll observations or by optional RegionScan collection; RegionScan remains useful evidence tooling but is not the only completion gate. The Prompt warns against declaring completion from a first viewport that plausibly has unseen lower content, while the framework keeps semantic judgment with the VLM.
- The first desktop Settings trial exposed a scheduler ordering bug before any GUI action: after the navigation sidebar completed, a generic `survey_needed=true` masked the concrete incomplete `About System Information Content` Region, and Qwen returned `action=null` repeatedly. `_page_survey_need` now prioritizes an active scrolling scan and a named incomplete Region before the generic follow-up flag, so the Agent receives a concrete survey target. This is a general task-fact ordering fix, not a Settings Prompt exception.

### 2026-08-04 - Give the two persistent tasks separate Prompt, fact and tool views

### 2026-08-04 - Minimize page updates, commit items independently, and remove the Region-bbox scroll gate

- Replaced the autonomous update payload with `page_update={page_name,regions,new_entries}` and no legacy adapter. The top-level `reason` and old `region_changes/entry_changes` names are gone. Region items no longer accept `scrollable/reason`; new entry items no longer accept `entry_id/control_type/current_value/temporary_bbox_1000/equivalence_reason/equivalent_destination_page`. The optional public geometry key is `bbox_1000`.
- New entry IDs are framework-owned. Accepted results return only `created_entries[{entry_id,target,region_name}]`; a model-supplied `entry_id` is an unsupported field. `equivalent_to_entry_id` can reference only a representative that existed before the update, while same-turn duplicates use `equivalent_occurrences`.
- After page ownership is accepted, Region items and then entry items commit independently. The compact `page_update_result` reports `accepted|partial|rejected`, accepted Region names, created entries and structured rejected items. A bad item does not roll back accepted siblings or stop an independent valid action. Entry references to unknown or rejected Regions fail only that entry. If a Region requests `coverage_complete=true` but an entry for that Region is rejected, its other facts remain accepted and completion is deferred with `coverage_completion_deferred`.
- Autonomous scroll still requires the current frame, a known current-page Region (or `full_screen`), a full-screen in-range point, direction, amount and valid argument types. It no longer requires a Region bbox or rejects a point for being outside that optional bbox. The factual result now includes the Region name, point, before/after frame IDs and moved state. This is the general regression for the five bbox-gate rejections observed in the earlier diagnostic; it does not special-case an application or Region.

### 2026-08-04 - Replace type-specific retry stops with one rejection boundary

- Removed the autonomous `MAX_INVALID_TURNS=3` stop and the repeated-finish `finish_no_progress` controller. Invalid Agent turns, rejected action/tool calls, fully rejected page updates and completion-gap rejections now share one turn-level boundary. One Agent turn counts at most once even when several returned items are rejected.
- A task is suspended after five rejected turns without a real GUI action or committed Page/Region/entry fact. Suspension is runtime-only and does not mark the task covered, inferred, unreachable or complete; the scheduler excludes it and selects another task. Ten such rejected turns across task switches stop the run as `framework_rejection_limit`. A real GUI action or committed Page/Region/entry/Page Identity review resets both counters; read-only queries, waits and record-error reports neither increase nor reset them.
- A registered Android entry that actually leaves the target application is now settled as a semantic-only terminal outcome without registering the external screenshot as a Page. The runtime reopens the target app through the existing data-preserving hard-reset path and continues from a fresh in-app screenshot. Unknown foreground ownership, an external frame without a pending action, or restart failure still stops safely.
- Restored the general positive entry boundary in the compact survey Prompt: controls that enter/switch independent function content, reveal hidden function content, or remove a function restriction are registered for later entry exploration. Controls that clearly leave the app keep only their semantics; the external surface is not registered or surveyed.

### 2026-08-05 - Expose one current exploration stage and project stage-local context

- Kept one autonomous main Agent and the existing `survey_page` / `explore_entry` task split. Each turn now appends only the current phase contract: `identify_page`, `review_identity`, `record_regions`, `survey_region`, `route_to_page`, `route_to_source`, `locate_entry`, `restore_state`, `rejection_recovery`, or `completion_ready`. Each short contract states the current visual goal and the framework-owned completion condition; it does not prescribe a fixed internal tool sequence or add an application/Page/control exception.
- The model-facing fact view is now phase-local. Identity and route phases receive the natural Page graph plus arrival context; Region phases receive the target Page/Region ledger; `locate_entry` receives the exact dispatched entry without the global graph; restore, rejection recovery, and completion receive their corresponding facts. A pending real action adds only its operation, target, source Page, optional entry ID, and the existing before/latest screenshots needed for settlement. `page_update` is accepted only in `record_regions/survey_region/rejection_recovery`; an early structure payload in another stage returns `page_update_outside_stage` and is resubmitted after the Region-stage context is available.
- Added a general stage transition gate. After accepting a Page identity/review, `page_update`, or pending-action settlement, the runtime resynchronizes the task. If the task or phase changed, it records `stage_transition`, saves the accepted evidence, and does not execute a tool proposed under the stale stage; the next turn receives a fresh stage Prompt. When the phase remains unchanged, navigation, scrolling, recovery, and other same-stage actions can still continue without a forced blank turn. Public tools remain visible under the existing task/platform catalog.

### 2026-08-05 - Separate Page scroll frontier review from Region coverage

- Kept the existing Region definition and ledger semantics. Autonomous `scroll` now accepts `{container_hint,point_1000,direction,amount}`: `container_hint` is a free-text description of the visible scroll recipient and no longer has to equal `full_screen` or a registered Region. The runtime still validates argument types and full-screen coordinates, records before/after evidence, and returns the hint with the factual scroll result.
- Added `survey_page(phase=review_scroll_frontier)` after every known Region is explicitly complete. The phase receives the full compact Page Region summary, optional Page-level `page_update.survey_memory`, and the neutral `last_scroll_result={container_hint,direction,point_1000,frame_changed}`. The Agent may close a visually clear Page with `action=null`; when uncertain it may issue one low-risk probe against the most plausible visible container. A changed probe can add Regions or entries, while an unchanged probe remains for Agent interpretation and may not be repeated unchanged. No top/middle/bottom buckets, DOM/scrollbar detector, forced physical-bottom rule, Region redefinition, or application-specific Prompt patch was added.
- Persisted `scroll_survey_memory` and `scroll_frontier_reviewed` in each Page's autonomous Region snapshot. Any accepted Region update or newly created entry invalidates the prior Page review; completion now reports an unreviewed Page scroll frontier as a factual gap. `page_update` is accepted in the new review phase, and a fully accepted observation-only turn closes it before entry scheduling.
- Focused Tier 3 offline verification: the four changed runtime files and five focused test files passed `py_compile`; `python -m pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp .codex-temp/pytest-page-scroll-frontier-final tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py` -> `145 passed in 7.14s`. No real Qwen call, browser-driven autonomous traversal, emulator action, full regression gate, or completion certification was run.

### 2026-08-05 - Define functional saturation for infinite feeds

  stabilized, the Agent may summarize the observed batches, repeated structure
  and remaining uncertainty in `survey_memory`, explicitly bind homogeneous
  instances to a representative, and complete the Region without claiming a
  physical bottom. One unchanged frame or temporary loading plateau is not
  sufficient evidence for this conclusion.

### 2026-08-05 - Require a readable reason on every autonomous model turn

- The main Agent response now always requires `screen/reason/action`. The
  top-level natural-language `reason` explains the current Page judgment, stage
  conclusion and current tool choice (or no action), while
  `previous_action.reason` remains scoped to the preceding real GUI result.
  The structured observation trace stores the top-level reason for later audit.
- Page Identity, Click Reviewer and pending specialist review results also
  require a non-empty reason. Runtime adapters reject or downgrade empty
  specialist explanations instead of accepting an unexplained decision. This
  is only a presence contract: the framework does not validate reason text by
  keyword or infer semantic truth from it.
- Closed a Page-settlement gap exposed by the Back fixture. A
  `no_visible_change` result is now compared against the Agent's reported
  `known/new` Page name even when that claimed known name is not registered;
  an unregistered shell/header label can no longer bypass
  `previous_action_page_conflict` and replace the source Page.
- Equivalent-entry guidance now prefers reuse when visible role, verified
  destination and recent result agree and there is no concrete source/state/
  context dependency. Merely appearing on a different Page is not a reason to
  click again. If the Agent directly probes a context-sensitive occurrence, its
  universal top-level reason records the visual evidence; no incident-specific
  `why_not_reuse` field was added.
- Verification: Python compilation passed for the three changed runtime
  modules. Focused autonomous protocol, action-tool, loop and integration tests
  passed (`172 passed in 7.15s`) using a workspace-local pytest temporary
  directory. The first pytest attempt was not counted because the default
  Windows pytest temp root was inaccessible; it also exposed one stale Prompt
  assertion, which was updated before the clean rerun. No live VLM, browser or
  Android traversal was run for this change.

### 2026-08-05 - Give shared Regions canonical local names and representative signatures

- Each Region now has a stable, locally discriminative natural-language name.
  The survey Prompt asks the Agent to describe identity-bearing components and
  roles rather than position, order, dynamic values, broad topic or a shared
  destination.
- `known_region_candidates` now exposes only `region_ref`, `canonical_name`,
  representative Page and the representative occurrence's component signature.
  Entry names from other occurrences are no longer unioned into the candidate,
  so an earlier bad merge cannot make itself appear visually consistent.
- Citing a known `region_ref` requires reusing its canonical Region name. A
  mismatched name is rejected with a readable correction: either resubmit the
  canonical name when the main components really correspond, or leave the ref
  empty and register a distinct Region. The framework still does not merge by
  strings, screenshots, coordinates or destination.
- Same-task Click Reviewer rejections stop appearing in model-facing history
  after a later real pointer action has been dispatched. They remain in the
  full trace for audit.
- Offline verification: Python compilation passed. Six focused history, Prompt,
  candidate and Region-write checks passed (`6 passed, 79 deselected`); the
  final adjacent autonomous loop, Region-ledger and tool-integration suite
  passed (`131 passed in 6.93s`).
- Saved-frame live `qwen3.7-plus` replay retained an important contrast. On the
  original Weekly review screenshot, Prompt/candidate wording alone still
  incorrectly cited `Team inbox`; after the general canonical-name rejection,
  the same model on the same screenshot correctly kept `Today's focus` as a new
  Region. Evidence is under
  `artifacts/replays/region_identity_neighbor_naming_20260805/`.
- The full deterministic Region fixture was then rerun with live
  `qwen3.7-plus`. All seven Region occurrences formed the intended four explicit
  Region groups and all four stable Region behaviors were verified. The run is
  still `incomplete`, not certified, because two repeated entry groups did not
  explicitly reuse a representative result; that is a separate entry-scheduling
  and model-choice problem, not hidden as a Region failure. Evidence is under
  `artifacts/runs/region_identity_neighbor_acceptance_20260805/20260805/quick-region-merge-script-acceptance-224128030/`.
- A user-requested independent full rerun under
  `artifacts/runs/region_identity_neighbor_repeat_20260805/20260805/quick-region-merge-script-acceptance-225712666/`
  again passed all four intended Region groups and all four verified behaviors,
  but only one of four repeated-entry groups reused a representative result, so
  the validator again reported `incomplete`. The Agent additionally grouped the
  Weekly review and Project workspace headers under canonical `Page header`:
  their title/button text differed but their title/subtitle/toggle component
  roles matched. This extra group is outside the fixture's intended four groups
  and is retained as a design-boundary example; no header-specific rule was
  added. The run used 12 GUI actions and 53 real Qwen calls and closed the local
  HTML environment normally.
- No Android traversal was run for this change.

### 2026-08-06 - Nest entry discovery under its owning Region and batch verified reuse

- The public autonomous Page update is now
  `page_update={page_name,regions:[{...,entries:[...]}]}`. An entry no longer
  repeats `region_name`; the runtime derives its Page/Region owner from the
  enclosing Region and keeps the existing flattened representation only as an
  internal write-path detail. Regions with no independent function entry may
  omit `entries` or submit an empty list.
- A shared-Region candidate now includes only its compact, already verified
  entry representatives: exact entry ID, target, source Page, latest real
  result and optional destination. During Page survey the Agent may bind a
  visible entry to one of those exact representatives with
  `equivalent_to_entry_id`; the framework creates the inferred entry/edge and
  does not dispatch a duplicate click. The reference must have existed before
  the Page update and still be verified. The framework does not infer
  equivalence by names, layout, coordinates or screenshot similarity.
- The survey Prompt now describes the Region as the candidate boundary for
  entry de-duplication and the parent item as the ownership contract. A stale
  sentence that restricted all Cross-Page reuse to a later `explore_entry`
  task was replaced: verified reuse may happen either in the owning Region's
  survey batch or later through `reuse_entry_result`.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the three changed runtime modules passed
  `py_compile`; autonomous loop, entry-ledger, Region-ledger and tool-integration
  tests passed (`158 passed in 6.79s`). This covers nested parsing, automatic
  owner binding, verified-only references, inferred destination propagation,
  candidate projection and the unchanged internal ledger/tool contracts.
- Real saved-frame replay on the original `Project workspace` screenshot
  succeeded in one Qwen call. The response registered three Region-owned
  entries, reused `ae4` for `Today's focus`, reused `ae1` for `Team inbox`, and
  kept `Team library` as a new entry. It used `4912` Prompt tokens and `770`
  completion tokens. Prompt, raw response and backend metadata are under
  `artifacts/replays/region_nested_entry_batch_20260806/project_workspace/`.
- The full deterministic Region-merge Local HTML fixture was then rerun with
  real `qwen3.7-plus` and certified all checks: three distinct Pages, seven
  Region occurrences, four explicit shared-Region groups, four explicit entry
  equivalence groups, one verified behavior per stable Region and all seven
  intended entry occurrences accounted for. Three repeated entries were
  created directly as inferred (`ae5 -> ae2`, `ae7 -> ae4`, `ae9 -> ae1`). The
  run used 12 GUI actions and 41 Qwen calls / 215690 total tokens, versus the
  previous saved baseline's 12 actions and 53 calls / 263287 tokens. Evidence is
  under
  `artifacts/runs/region_nested_entry_batch_acceptance_20260806/20260806/quick-region-merge-script-acceptance-002548989/`.
- The certified fixture still exposed two non-blocking quality traces retained

### 2026-08-06 - Keep the reviewed click target fixed during result settlement

- The two retained fixture traces shared one protocol fault rather than two
  control-specific failures. After Click Reviewer had fixed the executed
  control, the main Agent could still output a different
  `previous_action.target_name` by guessing backwards from the landing Page.
  The public field is removed: the runtime now settles against the target
  frozen in the pending action and accepted by Reviewer. The Agent only judges
  the visible outcome, explains it and reports whether that outcome belongs to
  the fixed control.
- Discovery clicks do not predict an unregistered destination. A coherent
  immediate transition is the reviewed control's result even when another
  entry reaches the same Page, and `task.page` remains the before-action source
  rather than a required landing Page. Truly unrelated visible results may
  still be reported with `matches_intent=false`; the framework does not compare
  labels or add an application, Page or control exception.
- A Reviewer-approved exact entry reported as `no_visible_change` now settles
  once as a no-effect probe even if incidental pixels differ between the before
  and after PNGs. The model must explain the likely reason and classify it as
  `temporarily_unavailable` or `not_interactive`; the unresolved entry becomes
  ineligible for automatic retry. PNG byte equality remains only for the
  existing hard contradiction where an exactly unchanged frame is reported as
  `changed`.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the two changed runtime modules passed
  `py_compile`; the complete autonomous loop, entry-ledger, Region-ledger and
  tool-integration suite passed (`159 passed in 7.81s`).
  Real saved-frame `qwen3.7-plus` replay then passed both original traces: the
  shared Weekly-review landing was accepted without renaming the reviewed
  `Browse review library` button, and the static `Project view` chip was
  classified once as `not_interactive` with no follow-up action. Final replay
  evidence is under
  `artifacts/replays/reviewer_bound_target_20260806/browse_library_shared_landing_r3/`
  and
  `artifacts/replays/reviewer_bound_target_20260806/project_view_no_effect_r2/`.
  No full fixture rerun, Android traversal or full framework regression gate
  was run for this change.

### 2026-08-06 - Let the main Agent filter page-survey entry candidates once

- Page survey still reports Region-owned `entries`, but they are now candidate
  observations rather than immediately scheduled traversal work. The runtime
  records valid Region facts, keeps the candidates without IDs, and enters one
  `review_entries` turn. The same main Agent sees the latest full screenshot and
  exact natural-language Region/target pairs, then keeps only controls that open
  or switch an independent Page, dialog, menu, panel or hidden functional
  Region. An empty selection is valid.
- Only kept candidates pass through the existing Page/Region/entry validation,
  receive framework entry IDs and enter the exploration queue. The framework
  does not classify targets by strings or run a specialist per button. Controls
  that only change a value or runtime/selection state, or operate the current
  function without revealing a new functional surface, can therefore be dropped
  before they create redundant click tasks.
- The review response schema requires `entry_review.independent_entries` and
  `action=null`; it does not expose `page_update` in that turn. Pending review is
  checkpointed, blocks completion, and is allowed to finish even when an
  explicitly positive GUI action cap has just been reached.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the changed runtime modules passed `py_compile`;
  the complete autonomous-loop suite passed (`90 passed in 5.52s`) and the
  autonomous tool-integration suite passed (`39 passed in 2.83s`) using isolated
  workspace-local pytest temp directories. A real one-call `qwen3.7-plus`
  replay on the saved GNOME Clocks Timer screenshot dropped all 14 duration
  preset and increment/decrement candidates, returned `action=null`, and used
  3392 Prompt plus 151 completion tokens. Prompt, raw response and backend
  metadata are under `artifacts/timer_entry_review_qwen_20260806/`.
- The first fresh GNOME Clocks run proved the filter path but exposed a separate
  survey omission: Qwen called the visible Alarms/Stopwatch/Timer tabs “global
  navigation” and did not propose them, so the framework correctly had no
  candidates from which to schedule those Pages and accepted a one-Page finish.
  The incomplete run is retained under
  `artifacts/desktop_clock_entry_filter_20260806/`; it finished with one Page,
  one reviewed functional entry, three GUI actions and no Page connections.
- The general survey contract now states that an entry is an executable graph
  edge from the current Page, not necessarily a control inside the main content
  Region. App-wide or top-level navigation that switches independent functional
  content must therefore be proposed; the already-selected no-op navigation item
  is omitted. This is not a Clocks/tab-name exception. On the exact failed World
  screenshot, a one-call real `qwen3.7-plus` replay then proposed Alarms,
  Stopwatch and Timer plus Add World Clock, while omitting the selected World
  tab. Evidence is under `artifacts/world_nav_survey_qwen_20260806/`.
- After the navigation-boundary clarification, the combined autonomous
  protocol, loop and tool-integration suite passed (`145 passed in 6.66s`).
- A second fresh Clocks run confirmed that the survey sentence alone remained
  stochastic: although the new rule was present in the actual Prompt, Qwen
  again said the tabs should be omitted to avoid Cross-Page duplication. The
  run is retained under
  `artifacts/desktop_clock_entry_filter_nav_prompt_20260806/` rather than being
  reported as traversal success.
- The existing focused `review_entries` turn now also completes candidate
  generation. Alongside exact candidate selection, the main Agent receives the
  Region names just recorded by the survey and returns `missed_entries` for
  clearly visible independent-function controls that the survey omitted. These
  additions cannot create a new Region and pass through the same Page/Region/
  entry validation as selected candidates. This remains one main-Agent call,
  not a per-control specialist or framework string classifier.
- Real one-call saved-frame replays passed both directions of the boundary. On
  World, the review kept Add World Clock and added Alarms, Stopwatch and Timer;
  on Timer, it dropped all 14 duration/value controls and added no internal
  entry. Evidence is under
  `artifacts/world_entry_review_completion_qwen_20260806/` and
  `artifacts/timer_entry_review_completion_qwen_20260806/`. The combined
  autonomous protocol, loop and tool-integration suite passed after this change

- A third fresh GNOME Clocks run exercised the completed review contract with

  Alarms, Stopwatch and Timer), all 12 directed cross-navigation connections,

### 2026-08-06 - Resurvey a functional surface revealed by a same-Page entry

- A verified, explicitly dispatched entry that visibly changes the interface
  while remaining on the same natural Page now creates one persisted
  `same_page_functional_surface_changed` survey request. The next
  `record_regions` turn is an incremental survey: prior completed Regions and
  entries remain intact, and the main Agent only records the newly revealed or
  visibly changed functional Region and its entries.
- Unbound navigation, recovery and incidental pointer actions do not create the
  request. A legal Region update closes it; completion and resume both retain
  the pending request until that evidence exists. This fixes the general menu,
  drawer or same-Page panel gap without matching application or control names
  and without reopening every old Region.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the changed runtime modules passed `py_compile`;
  the autonomous protocol, loop and tool-integration suite passed (`147 passed
  in 6.88s`). The new end-to-end fixture verifies one explicit menu entry click,
  same-Page settlement, a delta `record_regions` turn, preservation of the
  existing complete Region, registration of the revealed Region and accepted
  completion.
- A fresh GNOME Clocks run confirmed that the delta survey triggers on the real
  Add Alarm entry, then was deliberately stopped at the first collection issue.
  Qwen first named the foreground `New Alarm Modal` but incorrectly cited the
  background `Main Content Area` Region group. After the framework rejected the
  canonical-name conflict, Qwen briefly stated that the modal was distinct, but
  then reused the old canonical name; that structurally valid response was
  accepted and modal controls were attached to the wrong Region. The run was
  stopped with exit 143 and its verified task-owned container was removed;
  unrelated containers were preserved. Screenshot, complete Prompt/raw output,

  `artifacts/desktop_clock_same_page_region_issue_20260806/`. No special-case
  Prompt or corrective framework change was applied pending review.

### 2026-08-06 - Ground Click Reviewer target identity in the visible owner

- The stopped Clock run exposed a Click Reviewer identity error rather than a
  coordinate or Region error. The proposed normalized point mapped to a real
  Clocks title-bar control, while the requested software-update notification
  had no visible close affordance. Reviewer nevertheless copied the requested
  meaning onto that control and approved a click that closed the target app.
- The existing single Reviewer call is retained. Its Prompt now requires the
  actual control and direct visible owner to be identified before comparison
  with `target` and optional `requested_entry`. A similar icon in another
  window, dialog, card or row is a different target; an absent requested target
  cannot be completed from request text. Risk is judged from the observed
  control. Main-Agent `purpose/expected_result` and the normalized coordinate
  numbers are no longer sent to Reviewer.
- Reviewer now receives the untouched full screenshot first for ownership and
  a clean same-frame crop second whose exact center is the proposed operation
  pixel. The earlier red point/cross overlay was removed from model input so it
  cannot cover the native icon; the runtime still computes and executes the
  same pixel only after approval.
- Real `qwen3.7-plus` replay on the exact failed screenshot and request rejected
  all three final-contract calls with `target_matches_request=false` and placed
  the actual control on the Clocks window title bar rather than the
  notification. One call named the exact window close button and judged it
  unsafe; two called it the minimize control and judged it safe, but still
  rejected the target mismatch. This validates the execution veto, not stable
  fine-grained window-control naming. Prompt/raw outputs are under
  `artifacts/click_reviewer_owner_qwen_center_only_20260806/` and
  `artifacts/click_reviewer_owner_qwen_center_only_trials_20260806/`.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the two changed runtime modules compiled; the
  autonomous action-tool, loop and tool-integration suites passed (`167 passed
  in 7.44s`). No fresh Clock traversal, Region-review live acceptance or
  completion certificate was run after this change.

### 2026-08-06 - Require visible ownership before dismissing interference

- The two fresh Clock failures began one layer before Click Reviewer: the main
  Agent treated “dismiss the notification” as an intended effect, invented a
  notification close control that was not visible, and borrowed the Clocks
  title-bar X. The general protocol now permits interference handling only when
  the latest screenshot shows an interactive control directly owned by that
  interference. `click.target` must name the actual visible control and its
  direct visible owner, not an intended effect or invisible control.
- The existing single Click Reviewer remains the only model review. Its target
  is now explicitly a factual claim to verify rather than an intent to fulfill;
  a similar control or similar effect cannot substitute for a different visible
  owner. Approval requires a real center-hit control, exact control/owner match,
  and safety judged from that actual control. The prior scene-leading sentence
  that pre-labelled closing ads or update prompts as safe was removed.
- No application, notification, title-bar coordinate or target-name rule was
  added.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the three directly affected runtime modules
  compiled; the autonomous protocol, action-tool, loop and tool-integration
  suites passed (`183 passed in 7.10s`). Three real read-only
  `qwen3.7-plus` replays on the exact second-live-failure frame, target and point
  all rejected the Clocks title-bar close control with
  `target_matches_request=false` and `risk=unsafe`. Complete Prompt/raw output
  is under `artifacts/click_reviewer_factual_target_qwen_20260806/`; this does
  not yet validate main-Agent behavior or a fresh traversal.
- A fresh js1 Clock run exercised the original interference boundary. With the
  software-update notification still visible, the main Agent explicitly stated
  that it did not obscure the current target and did not request a dismiss
  click. It instead named the visible green Add World Clock control and direct
  owner; Reviewer accepted the exact control, the modal opened, and a separately
  reviewed Cancel returned safely. After the notification disappeared, the
  visible Alarms tab was correctly reviewed and clicked, reaching Alarms; Add
  Alarm was then reviewed and opened New Alarm. The original window-close
  failure did not recur.
- Monitoring initially stopped immediately after the Alarms review because the
  compact JSON was mistakenly read as if the notification were still present.
  Pulled before/after PNGs proved the notification had disappeared and the point
  was centered on the real visible Alarms tab. A resume restored the saved graph
  and reached Alarms again, but could not preserve the transient New Alarm modal
  across the new VM; it later proposed a vague duplicate `Leftmost navigation
  tab` alongside the existing World entry. The resume was stopped at that new
  ledger-quality issue. Both run-owned containers were verified by creation time
  and qcow mount and removed; the ten pre-existing containers were untouched.
  Combined evidence is under
  `artifacts/desktop_clock_factual_target_live_resume_20260806/`, with resume
  launcher logs under
  `artifacts/desktop_clock_factual_target_resume_logs_20260806/`.
- This is live regression evidence that the initial notification no longer
  causes the Agent/Reviewer pair to close Clocks. It is not a complete Clock

### 2026-08-06 - Ignore non-blocking temporary interference

- The main-Agent rule now states the decision boundary before dismissal:
  temporary UI that does not obscure, block or prevent reliable judgment of the
  current stage target is ignored while exploration continues. The Agent must
  not close transient UI merely to clean up the screen. Only interference that
  actually affects the current stage proceeds to the existing visible-owner
  requirement before any action is proposed.
- This is a Prompt clarification only; no new tool, state, reviewer or
  application-specific rule was added.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the three directly affected runtime modules
  compiled; the autonomous protocol, action-tool, loop and tool-integration
  suites passed (`183 passed in 7.17s`). `git diff --check` reported only
  existing line-ending warnings. No additional live run was started for this
  one-line decision-priority clarification; the immediately preceding fresh
  Clock run already exhibited the intended behavior by ignoring the
  non-blocking update notification and continuing with Add World Clock.

### 2026-08-06 - Do not backfill obscured aliases as missed entries

- The stopped Clock resume had already exposed `Top Navigation Bar` and exact
  candidates `World tab`, `Stopwatch tab`, and `Timer tab` to the main Agent.

  nevertheless added `Leftmost navigation tab` through the free-form
  `missed_entries` channel. Exact-key validation accepted the different string,
  leaving both `World tab` and its positional alias task-eligible.
- Entry review now requires every missed item to be visibly and semantically
  distinct from every candidate already exposed for the same Region. A
  partially obscured view, alias, alternate wording, or position-only name does
  not create a new entry; insufficient identity evidence is deferred until an
  unobscured screenshot. The existing single main-Agent review and free-form
  discovery path remain; no string-similarity classifier, bbox rule, extra
  specialist or application-specific navigation rule was added.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the two changed runtime modules compiled; the
  autonomous protocol, loop, tool-integration and action-tool suites passed
  (`183 passed in 7.32s`). Three real `qwen3.7-plus` entry-review calls built
  from the current source Prompt/schema on the exact obscured Alarms frame all
  returned `missed_entries=[]`; none recreated the positional alias. Two kept
  only the unobscured Add Alarm candidate and one also kept all known navigation
  candidates, so candidate retention under occlusion remains model-dependent.
  Complete Prompt/raw output is under
  `artifacts/entry_review_obscured_alias_qwen_20260806/`; this is saved-frame
  evidence, not a fresh traversal result.
- A subsequent fresh js1 Clock run started from an empty ledger. It correctly
  reviewed and clicked the real Add World Clock button, opened the city-search
  dialog, reviewed that dialog's own Cancel control, and returned to World; the

  alias was created. However, the initial unobscured screenshot showed all four

  Entry review explicitly excluded the top navigation and returned no missed
  entries, contrary to the current global-navigation rule, then the Agent
  submitted `finish_exploration`.
