  `artifacts/desktop_clock_entry_alias_live_20260806/`.

### 2026-08-06 - Independently review new Region partitions and Agent reasons

- The fresh Clock run showed that entry review could not recover when the main
  Agent omitted an entire Region: the initial screenshot clearly exposed the
  World / Alarms / Stopwatch / Timer navigation group, but the Agent proposed
  only `World Clock List Area` and justified the omission by calling the tabs
  global switching. The existing Region-equivalence step was a same-main-Agent
  correction triggered only by an explicit `equivalent_to_region_ref`; it had
  no proposal to inspect when a Region was never reported.
- New Region occurrences and new shared-Region bindings now pass one stateless
  Region Reviewer before ledger mutation. It receives the latest complete
  screenshot, current and proposed Regions/entries, referenced Region candidate
  signatures and verified entries, plus the main Agent's top-level reason. One
  structured verdict checks visible functional-component coverage, every
  proposed equivalence, and whether the Agent reason is consistent with the
  screenshot and traversal rules.
- Approval requires complete coverage, a consistent reason, and a decisive
  `same|different` result for every referenced Region. `same` preserves the
  ref; `different` clears it and records an independent Region. Missing
  coverage, contradictory reasoning, uncertain equivalence, malformed reviewer
  output or an unavailable Reviewer fails closed and returns the main Agent to
  Region correction without writing the proposal. A single button/icon/label
  cannot be reported as a missing Region; it is only evidence for its coherent
  toolbar, navigation group or content component. Ordinary updates to an
  already bound Region do not add a Reviewer call.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the changed runtime modules compiled; autonomous
  Region, loop, tool-integration, protocol and action-tool suites passed (`197
  passed in 7.10s`). The directly affected Region/loop/tool-integration suites
  also passed after the final component-group clarification (`144 passed in
  6.90s`).
  Three real `qwen3.7-plus` calls on the original Clock screenshot, original
  wrong reason and incomplete proposal all returned `reject`,
  `coverage_complete=false`, `reason_consistent=false`, and identified
  `Top Navigation Bar` as the missing Region. Complete Prompt/raw output is
  under `artifacts/region_partition_reviewer_qwen_final_20260806/`. This is
  saved-frame evidence.
- A subsequent fresh live Clock traversal with real `qwen3.7-plus` proposed
  both `Top Navigation Bar` and `World Clock List Area` on its first survey;
  the independent Reviewer returned `approve`, `coverage_complete=true`, and
  `reason_consistent=true`. Entry review retained Alarms, Stopwatch, Timer, and
  Add World Clock, so the prior "global navigation need not be recorded" error
  did not recur. The run then exposed a separate recovery failure: while a
  transient update notification covered Alarms, Click Reviewer safely rejected
  the window-close misidentification and four other invalid/covered proposals,
  but the main Agent repeatedly invented a notification `X` instead of waiting
  for the overlay to disappear. The run was intentionally stopped with exit 143,
  has no `completion.json`, and is not a completed traversal. Evidence and the
  explicit screenshot-persistence limitation are under
  `artifacts/desktop_clock_region_reviewer_live_20260806/`.

### 2026-08-06 - Add bounded on-demand interruption handling and desktop hover

- The main autonomous Agent no longer invents a dismissal action when a
  temporary foreground surface blocks the current task. It only calls
  `handle_interruption` with the visible suspected surface and a concrete
  obstruction reason. Non-blocking surfaces are still ignored, so ordinary
  frames add no specialist call.
- A stateless Interruption Handler receives the latest complete screenshot,
  current task, and main-Agent observation, then selects exactly one of
  `ignore|wait|hover|click|back|unresolved`. Desktop `hover` moves to a point
  inside the currently visible interference surface, waits briefly, and saves a
  fresh screenshot. The whole notification/banner/dialog/card cannot classify
  itself as a close control, and a Handler click still passes the independent
  Click Reviewer before dispatch. Handler output is framework-bound rather than
  rewritten by the main Agent; the same task has a three-round limit.
- Focused Tier 3 verification: the changed runtime and test modules compiled;
  autonomous action-tool, loop, and tool-integration suites passed (`180 passed
  in 7.40s`). Tests cover desktop-only hover, hover followed by a fresh-frame
  owner-visible close click, Click Reviewer participation, false close-control
  claims, whole-surface click rejection, the round limit, and ordinary dynamic
  catalogs.
- The first real saved-frame Qwen call on the original Clock failure frame
  exposed a generic ownership error: it mislabeled the whole notification card
  as a close control and guessed that clicking it might clear the overlay. The
  independent Click Reviewer would still have rejected that click. After the
  visual ownership rule was made explicit, a second real `qwen3.7-plus` call on
  the identical frame returned `strategy=hover`, a point inside the visible
  notification, and `target_is_close_control=false`, explaining that no owned
  close control was yet visible. Complete Prompt/raw output is under
  `artifacts/interruption_handler_qwen_20260806_v2/`. This is saved-frame
  evidence only; no fresh VM hover or completed Clock traversal is claimed.
- A subsequent fresh Clock run exercised the on-demand boundary with real
  Qwen. The initial software-update notification did not cover `Add World
  Clock`, so the main Agent explicitly ignored it and no Handler call was made.
  After that entry opened `Add a New World Clock`, the modal did block the
  current World survey: the main Agent called `handle_interruption`, the Handler
  chose the modal-owned visible Cancel control, the independent Click Reviewer
  approved it, and the real click dismissed only the modal. Clocks remained
  open and later navigation reached Alarms, Stopwatch, and Timer.
- The notification disappeared before it blocked a later target, so this live
  run did not execute hover and does not replace the saved-frame hover evidence.
  It was stopped with exit 143 and no `completion.json` after a separate issue:
  once the hamburger menu was open, Click Reviewer repeatedly interpreted
  proposed dropdown-item points as the still-hovered Menu control and rejected
  them. No rejected click was dispatched. Evidence is under
  `artifacts/desktop_clock_interruption_handler_live_20260806/`. The single
  run-owned container was verified by its seeded-qcow mount and removed; the
  ten pre-existing containers were untouched.

### 2026-08-07 - Separate Entry review and limit traversal to primary app functions

  Entry Reviewer call. The Reviewer receives the latest complete screenshot,
  exact Region/target candidates, existing entries, and the main Agent's
  proposal reason; it returns authoritative keep/add lists plus
  `reason_consistent` and a readable reason. This call occupies the former
  review slot, so it does not increase the model-call count.
- Changed the toolbar rule from automatic retention to mandatory review: only
  minimize, maximize/restore, and close are excluded before candidate review;
  every other visible interactive toolbar control must be considered, but is
  retained only when it serves the application's primary functionality.

  carry core operations are excluded without naming any application or menu
  item in the Prompt. Unknown containers may still be explored once so their
  branches can be judged from visible evidence.

  specialist's reason-consistency judgment in `entry_review_result`.

### 2026-08-07 - Let the main Agent decide entry retry versus termination

- Removed the autonomous runtime gate that forced every Reviewer-confirmed
  `no_visible_change` entry click into a terminal failure classification. The
  framework no longer infers retry versus termination from screenshot pixels.

  Agent's explicit `temporarily_unavailable` or `not_interactive` decision
  terminates its dispatch.
- Clarified the main-Agent settlement Prompt that dismissing a foreground menu
  or temporary surface without activating the underlying entry is
  `outcome=changed`, `matches_intent=false`, and `failure_kind=null`. The main
  Agent may then choose the next action from the clean frame. Click Reviewer
  behavior and call count are unchanged.

  the foreground surface, the main Agent keeps `matches_intent=false`, then a

### 2026-08-07 - Replace main-Agent Region override with bounded discussion

- Superseded the direct main-Agent Region adjudication above. Region Reviewer
  now owns the final acceptance decision; `review_region_record` and the
  `main_agent_override` path were removed.
- Reviewer rejection preserves the complete proposal, verdict, frame, and
  `review_attempt`. The next main-Agent turn receives that full correction and
  must resubmit a complete partition with a reason that accepts or disputes the
  prior findings. The next Reviewer request includes the complete
  `previous_review`, `review_attempt`, and `review_attempt_limit`, so it reviews
  the response rather than repeating a stateless first pass.
- Attempt 1 is the initial review; attempts 2 and 3 are the two discussion
  reviews. Any approval commits the current complete proposal. If attempt 3 is
  reject/uncertain, structurally invalid, unavailable, or raises, runtime stores
  `region_review_exhausted` and `review_exhausted=true`; the proposal remains
  uncommitted and later submissions do not invoke a fourth Reviewer.
- New submissions replace the provisional proposal rather than unioning with
  it. Verified Entries, real actions, and graph edges remain outside this
  provisional discussion.

  contradictions are now reported as concrete partition issues instead of a
  second boolean verdict.

  component groups. It does not audit entry coverage button by button and does
  not turn one omitted button into a missing Region; visible entry omissions
  remain the Entry Reviewer's responsibility. Single controls belong to their

### 2026-08-08 - Define Region by reusable functional identity

- Unified the main-Agent survey, legacy Region-equivalence correction, Region
  Reviewer, and `page_update` schema wording around one contract: a Region is a
  stable functional component identity used for global deduplication and entry
  identity/result reuse across Page occurrences. It is not a rectangle,
  generic content container, position, or layout slot.
- Reuse now asks whether the candidate's entry identities and verified results
  remain accurate on the current occurrence. Concrete inner components, entry
  semantics, and results must correspond. A shared central workspace or outer
  container cannot compensate for different inner controls or entry functions;
  every Reviewer `same` reason must name a same-function concrete component or
  entry from the candidate signature and current screenshot.

### 2026-08-08 - Require Region-scoped entry coverage evidence

- A Region that asks for `coverage_complete=true` now remains incomplete until
  the independent Entry Reviewer accepts a full-screen coverage audit. This also
  applies when the main Agent proposed no entries; ordinary candidate batches
  still use their existing single Reviewer call.
- Entry review reads the current full screenshot, exact committed Regions, main-
  Agent candidates, and known entries. It must audit every requested Region and
  may only reuse the exact approved Region names. This path does not use
  OmniParser, local control boxes, SoM, or a second visual inventory.
- Region identity remains a hard boundary. Formal partition and cross-Page
  equivalence remain exclusively the main Agent proposal plus Region Reviewer
  approval path. Entry Reviewer cannot create, split, merge, rename, or rebind
  Regions; runtime exposes only Regions whose State and Registry refs agree.
- Staging now normalizes every Region and entry before changing coverage intent.
  Invalid items keep exact corrections while valid siblings preserve the
  existing partial-commit contract. Entry review writes entries only and cannot
  replay or mutate the formal Region partition; rejected entries keep their
  owning audited Region incomplete.
- Region State and Registry binding is transactional. A failed cross-Page group
  merge restores all in-memory Region states, including current frame and bbox
  geometry, as well as the Registry.
- Accepted per-Region audits and their coverage basis persist in
  `autonomous_regions.json` schema v3. A dropped exact `(Page, Region, target)`
  candidate resolves only its matching stale correction and does not clear
  unrelated corrections.

### 2026-08-08 - Tighten autonomous Region partition Prompt on multi-app frames

- The main-Agent `record_regions` Prompt now first selects the target app's
  active interaction surface, excludes desktop/other-app/window-management
  chrome, uses coarse independently functioning component boundaries, merges
  dependent visual fragments, and requires a current-frame `bbox_1000` for
  every newly proposed Region. Its final preflight removes known invalid
  Regions instead of relying on Reviewer correction.
- Region Reviewer now re-evaluates every attempt from the latest screenshot,
  accepts app-owned foreground menus/dialogs/search or command surfaces, checks
  approximate sibling geometry, and deletes entry-only issues before deciding.
  The runtime schema remains optional for bbox so existing Region updates and
  checkpoints keep their current contract; this is a Prompt behavior change.
- Real `qwen3.7-plus` saved-frame A/B used the same 54 simulator frames and the
  same thinking=false, temperature=0.5, top_p=0.9, max_tokens=1500 settings.
  On the 44-frame provisional clean subset, predicted Regions moved from
  149 versus 77 gold occurrences to 127 versus 77; exact-count frames improved

### 2026-08-08 - Reject sibling-only writes for an assigned Region survey

- `survey_region` now has a runtime task-alignment guard. When the scheduler
  assigns Region A, a `page_update` that touches only sibling Regions is
  rejected as `survey_region_target_missing` before Region Review or ledger
  mutation. The original correction for A is preserved, so accepting a
  repeatedly re-reported sibling can no longer manufacture stable progress.
- A valid update may still include A and any visible sibling Regions in the
  same batch. The change therefore preserves the existing batched-Region
  contract and adds no application, page, control, or incident-specific Prompt
  rule.
- The rejection is a normal `page_update_result(status=rejected)` and enters the
  shared 5-per-task/10-per-run no-progress boundary. It does not create a second
  task ledger or a special Clock recovery path.
- Tier 2 focused verification:
  `python -m pytest tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py -q --basetemp C:\tmp\pytest-task-alignment-suite-20260808-a -p no:cacheprovider`
  -> `170 passed in 6.95s`; the dedicated call-path test also passed after it
  was strengthened to invoke `_review_region_proposal` directly. `py_compile`
  passed for `autonomous_loop.py` and `git diff --check` passed for the changed
  runtime and test files.
- A first isolated live Clock rerun deployed the exact task-alignment source to
  js1 and preserved the ten pre-existing containers. It dismissed the Software
  Updates notification through a hover-revealed close control, but a Region
  discussion exhausted on the earlier obstructed frame stayed permanently
  final after the unobstructed screenshot arrived. The framework stopped itself
  with `all_remaining_work_suspended` after 20 successful model calls, 14 main
  decisions, three Region Reviews and two real GUI actions; no
  `completion.json` was produced. This run did not reach a `survey_region`
  sibling-only proposal, so it validates the new earlier recovery blocker, not
  the task-alignment guard live. Evidence is under
  `artifacts/clock_task_alignment_live_20260808_202120_evidence/`.
- A final Region rejection now stays final on the same screenshot, while a
  different PNG byte frame reopens only that page's exhausted top-level Region
  discussion at attempt 1. The runtime records
  `region_review_discussion_reopened`; unrelated Region/entry corrections are
  preserved. This is visual-evidence recovery, not an application-specific
  retry or an unlimited same-frame Reviewer loop.
- Tier 3 focused verification after both runtime changes:
  `python -m pytest tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py -q --basetemp C:\tmp\pytest-region-review-reopen-suite-20260808-a -p no:cacheprovider`
  -> `170 passed in 6.91s`. The focused same-frame-final/new-frame-reopen and
  sibling-only/aligned-batch tests passed; `py_compile` passed for
  `autonomous_loop.py`, and `git diff --check` passed for the runtime and tests.

### 2026-08-08 - Close stateless Entry-review rejection feedback

- A rejected Entry Reviewer result now persists its exact framework
  `message/feedback` under the existing pending review and includes it as
  `previous_rejection` in the next stateless Reviewer request. The field is
  removed from the stable rejection-progress token, so merely receiving or
  changing feedback cannot manufacture exploration progress.
- `review_entry_record(operation=correct)` now resolves only the matching
  `(Page, Region, target)` page-update correction for each explicitly dropped
  same-frame deferred candidate. Unrelated corrections and all formal graph
  facts remain unchanged.
- Region Reviewer equivalence expectations are projected only for real Region
  candidate refs. An invented ref no longer creates an impossible Reviewer
  check set; the unchanged proposal still reaches normal partial validation and
  receives `invalid_region_ref` on that exact Region item.
- Tier 3 focused verification:
  `python -m pytest tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py -q --basetemp C:\tmp\pytest-entry-review-loop-suite-20260808-a -p no:cacheprovider`
  -> `172 passed in 6.97s`. Four dedicated tests for prior rejection replay,
  stable progress, exact correction cleanup and unknown-ref projection passed
  `4 passed in 1.04s`. `py_compile` and `git diff --check` passed. An initial
  sandboxed attempt failed only because the isolated `C:\tmp` pytest directory
  was not writable; the approved rerun supplied the behavioral result.
- An isolated js1 Clock run deployed exact hashes, began with ten pre-existing
  containers and no other traversal, and released its own container with the
  same ten container IDs/names remaining. It reached World, Alarms, Stopwatch
  and Timer; all 14 Region occurrences completed with accepted
  `visual_entry_reviewer` audits and no pending correction or review.

  internal controls back into the task ledger. The graph expanded to 39 entries
  (27 verified, 2 inferred, 9 unresolved, 1 discovered); the remaining eligible
  `Name input field` task reached five rejected no-progress turns and suspended.
  Final `stop_reason=all_remaining_work_suspended`, exit code 0, no
  `completion.json`.
- Runtime totals were 288 backend calls (287 successful, one recovered main
  parse error), 53 GUI actions, 1,611,700 prompt tokens and 71,794 completion

  not Region geometry. Evidence is under

### 2026-08-08 - Re-review an exact false-positive Entry before retiring it

  to the exact active `explore_entry` on its registered source Page. Runtime

  Entry Reviewer on the latest screenshot.

  reopen whole-page coverage. A retained or deferred candidate, inconsistent

### 2026-08-09 - Bound Entry-review disagreements and keep inter-Agent feedback natural

- Any Entry Reviewer omission of a submitted main-Agent candidate, or an
  inconsistent judgment of the main-Agent reason, is now a disagreement rather
  than an accepted audit. Candidate changes, Reviewer additions, coverage and
  matching corrections remain uncommitted until the revised full proposal and
  Reviewer verdict agree.
- The same main Agent receives the complete kept, added, dropped and deferred
  lists, deferred Regions, reason-consistency judgment and Reviewer reason in
  natural language. It may revise the proposal twice; a third unresolved review
  fails closed for that visible evidence and a fourth same-evidence submission
  does not call the Reviewer again.
- Discussion identity is Page plus disputed owner Regions and persisted
  `entry_review_evidence_generation`, not screenshot hash, action budget or the
  exact sibling audit set. Only a settled real GUI action reported changed with
  byte-different before/after PNGs advances the generation. Dynamic pixels,
  no-effect actions, sibling audit changes and resume therefore do not reset the
  three rounds.
- A one-candidate `report_record_error(kind=entry)` review is advisory dispute
  evidence only. One omitted or dropped registered Entry no longer calls
  `mark_reclassified_non_entry`; the formal Entry remains task-eligible and in
  the completion gaps until an ordinary Page Entry proposal reaches agreement
  or new GUI evidence is obtained.
- Model-facing requests for the main Agent, Page Identity, Region Reviewer,
  Entry Reviewer, Interruption Handler and Click Reviewer now project complete
  natural-language facts and correction directions. Internal status/outcome
  codes, retry fields, review-attempt objects, Region acceptance markers and
  backend exit details remain in trace/checkpoint/debug only. Stable
  Page/Region/Entry identifiers and specialist output schemas remain structured
  where exact binding is required.
- Verification was offline Tier 3 only. `python -m py_compile

### 2026-08-09 - Reuse active-surface semantics and close certified Entry owner corrections

- Region Reviewer now reuses the existing shared current-operable-interface
  definition. Passive status, explanation or feedback that can be ignored
  without opening a new operation path is outside the active surface and cannot
  become a Region. When it prevents reliable review of the proposed component
  boundary, the decision becomes uncertain instead of creating a Region for the
  occluder; hidden entry coverage remains the Entry Reviewer's responsibility.
  Transience alone neither proves nor disproves Region identity.
- Bbox review now follows the pixels actually enclosed on the latest screenshot;
  a proposal's summary/reason cannot self-certify that window or external
  surfaces were excluded.
- The Reviewer contract now states that `bbox_1000` is normalized
  `[left, top, right, bottom]`, not `[x, y, width, height]`.
- Runtime now rejects only a newly created Region that omits `bbox_1000`;
  valid siblings still commit and existing Region updates may omit geometry.
  Stale fixtures were updated, including removal of the obsolete assumption
  that a new Region could be registered without a bbox.
- The same Reviewer call now receives both the unmodified screenshot and a
  deterministic overlay of its proposed Region boxes. This adds no detector or
  additional VLM call.
- Compressed the repeated Entry-versus-partition wording into one ownership
  rule. Prompt tests now lock the shared semantic contract and schema rather
  than every duplicate sentence.
- A consistent, non-deferred, non-rejected complete Entry audit now clears only
  stale `new_entry/region_name/unknown_region_reference` corrections in the
  certified Region. This closes target-renaming loops such as `+ button` to
  `Add button (+)` without fuzzy matching or clearing another Region/error type.
- Final Tier 3-focused verification: `py_compile` passed for the two runtime
  modules and their tests; the four directly affected autonomous runtime,
  Region, protocol and integration test files -> `211 passed`; scoped
  `git diff --check` passed.
- Real `qwen3.7-plus` saved-frame validation used an unobstructed Clock frame and
  the exact earlier Clock frame containing the software-update banner. With the
  final overlay and coordinate contract, invalid full-screen Header geometry was
  rejected 2/2, corrected geometry approved 2/2, the passive banner was omitted
  from the Region partition 2/2, and proposals registering it as a Region were
  rejected 2/2. The Entry Reviewer retained the three visible candidates while
  deferring `Header Bar` coverage on the obstructed frame 3/3. Raw prompts and
  replies are under `artifacts/clock_region_surface_replay_qwen_20260809/`.
- This is saved-frame real-model evidence, not a fresh Clock traversal or
  VM/device acceptance. The full framework regression gate was not run.

### 2026-08-09 - Remove the obsolete direct-action Entry writer

- The only production caller was already guarded by a non-empty explicit
  `entry_id`. It now calls `begin_explicit_action()` directly. Unbound pointer
  actions still execute and retain before/after and ActionEdge evidence, but
  they have no path that can create a formal Entry.

  field. New pending-action snapshots contain only the action ID, exact Entry
  ID and frame ID.

  `begin_registered_action()`. The current runtime has one Entry execution
  path: bind the exact registered ID, use the latest Agent-proposed action, and
  pass Click Review. Temporary Entry bbox remains observation evidence only
  and is cleared on resume.
- Removed the uncalled `mark_reclassified_non_entry()` state writer. Exact
  Entry disputes remain advisory and cannot retire formal work outside the
  normal bounded Page Entry reconciliation.
- Removed the uncalled ledger-wide `agent_reference_view()`. The runtime's
  candidate and history projections remain task-local and Region-owned; there
  is no second compact Entry inventory.
- Removed the obsolete autonomous `inspect_region()` formal Entry writer and
  its `_merge_observation()` implementation. Formal creation and observation
  refresh now have one path: reviewed main-Agent Page updates enter
  `record_agent_update()`. Model-supplied IDs, statuses, control values and
  equivalence reasons cannot mutate the ledger; action/result facts remain
  framework-owned.
- Removed the inspector-only `StatusUpdate`, `PossibleDuplicate` and
  serializable `InspectionDelta` structures. The remaining `EntryUpdateDelta`
  reports only framework-created and exact-matched Entry IDs.

### 2026-08-09 - Connect autonomous Entry attempts to Phase 1 effect evidence

- Future autonomous action evidence now persists the active
  `exploration_task_id`. A formal explicit Entry action also persists its exact
  Entry ID, owner Region name/ref and Entry discovery source, so new artifacts
  no longer need the historical snapshot bridge before probe induction.
- The existing main-Agent settlement response now has one required nullable
  `previous_action.state_change` field. A non-null report contains only
  an exact approved `region_name`, `capability_name`, a stable observable `fact`,
  and visible before/after values; every field is consumed by the effect
  observation. The arrival context exposes exact source-Page Region names.
  Navigation, menus, dialogs, Region reveals, passive changes and unobserved
  values must use null. This reuses the existing before/after VLM turn and adds
  no model call.
- Runtime appends a supported Region-scoped `state_change` only when the
  action binds a formal Entry, its owner `region_ref` exists, the reported
  effect Region resolves to a separately committed `region_ref`, the main Agent
  says the result matched, the PNG bytes changed and settlement remains on the
  same canonical Page. The Entry owner remains the action source and may differ

### 2026-08-09 - Probe Region-internal operations without creating Entries

- Added one `probe_region` exploration task after all Page surveys and formal
  Entry tasks are exhausted. It is keyed by canonical `region_ref`, dispatches
  one Region occurrence at a time and is skipped by target-edge diagnostics.
- A Region probe reuses the existing main Agent, action validation, Click
  Reviewer, real GUI execution and before/after settlement. Pointer operations
  must lie inside the exact assigned Region and keep `entry_id` empty, so
  Start/Pause/Reset-style internal operations cannot pollute the Entry ledger.
- One task ID groups a multi-action recipe. A supported visible state change or
  a Reviewer-bound exact no-visible-change result closes that attempt; a new
  ordinal is then scheduled for the same Region. The main Agent calls
  `complete_region_probe(reason)` only when no additional safe, visible core
  operation remains. Known supported capabilities are returned in compact
  natural language on later probe turns.
- Supported effects may resolve to a different approved Region on the same Page
  from the action-source Region. Capability induction now accepts either a
  formal Entry start or a formal Region-probe start; Region starts retain the
  v1 `entry_surfaces` shape with an empty `entry_id` and non-empty `region_ref`
  instead of adding a parallel schema.
- Region-probe progress is persisted by canonical ref in
  `autonomous_regions.json` schema v4, survives resume and participates in
  completion gaps. Shared Region occurrences are probed once globally.
- Removed obsolete autonomous `TaskProgress` and `LoopAssessment` default
  objects and their trace-only copies. They had already left the public
  response schema and had no scheduler, completion or recovery consumer.
  The still-consumed temporary-state/resume path was deliberately left intact.

### 2026-08-09 - Feed capability evidence gaps back to Region probing

- Open Region-probe candidates now reuse the exact Phase 1 capability induction
  instead of maintaining a second runtime grouping rule. The induction API has
  one read-only `region_probe_source_ref` filter, so scheduling is based on the
  Region that owns the operation even when its visible effect is reported in a
  different formal Region.
- Candidate priority is fixed and evidence-driven: `discovered` first,
  `executable` second, no supported capability third, and already
  `effect_verified` work last. A completed Region remains excluded and is never
  reopened by this ranking.
- The same result is projected to the existing main Agent as a natural-language
  task reason and per-capability evidence note. One safe reversible repeat may
  close a single-evidence gap; repeated but unstable evidence permits at most one
  discriminating check; already verified recipes are not repeated merely to add
  counts. Homogeneous choices must not be enumerated. The Agent can still close
  the Region with a concrete visual reason when a repeat is not safely possible.

  provisional duplicate effect grouping and its abstract
  `formal_region_awaits_internal_capability_probe` reason were removed before
  acceptance.

### 2026-08-09 - Compile autonomous ledgers into the collection-ready bundle

- Kept the physical contract split instead of introducing another unified JSON.
  The autonomous raw `graph.json`, Region/Entry sidecars and optional effect
  batch are compiler inputs. The production handoff is an annotated schema-v3
  graph, an independent `gui_rewalk.capability_graph.v1` whose exact SHA-256
  `source_graph_digest` binds that graph, and the existing node/screenshot/trace
  artifacts.
- `run_capability_induction.py` now compiles formal autonomous inventory in the
  same annotated copy used for effect induction. It first attaches missing
  source-Region refs, then projects formal Region occurrences to
  `semantic_blocks` and formal Entries to semantic-only Elements with
  `uid=autonomous-entry:<entry_id>`. It never overwrites the raw graph and
  never copies observation-local bbox/frame evidence.
- The projection is deliberately narrow: it accepts only current empty-inventory
  `luna_autonomous` producer States, requires no pending Entry action, and
  requires every sidecar Page to map to exactly one State. It does not mirror
  Entry completion/inference flags into `visited/covered`, merge an arbitrary
  existing inventory, or invent Page Variants. Ambiguous and multi-State Pages
  fail closed.
- Resume now preserves producer-issued `page_identity_version=external`
  Page/Variant IDs after semantic inventory projection. Strict semantic identity
  versions keep their existing facts/hash validation.
- M13 capability-graph hydration now requires a valid digest that exactly matches
  the supplied graph bytes. It also verifies the declared Page/Variant/State,
  formal source Region and unique projected Entry before any GUI startup.
  Autonomous sidecars are not an M13 input. The existing `full_screen` action
  Region sentinel is treated only as a no-Region grounding wildcard; all other
  Region constraints remain exact and every action still re-grounds on the
  fresh screenshot.
- Added one producer-consumer gate that writes a current autonomous-shaped raw
  graph and sidecars, compiles a repeated `state_change` capability, loads the
  annotated graph, checks Attempt/effect refs and digest, generates one task, and
  passes `run_visual_collection.py --validate-only`.

  current capability-induction document. Its four material Variants, stable
  Region/Element identities, query/object effects and cleanup edge are the target
  contract, not current behavior. The regression fixture constructs all four

### 2026-08-10 - Preserve autonomous material Variants as separate States

- Extended the constrained main-Agent screen observation and the existing
  Page Identity specialist result with a Page-scoped material Variant name,
  `known|new|uncertain` identity and screenshot-grounded visible predicates.
  A Page remains a stable functional location; a Variant is only an operable
  condition that changes available actions, preconditions, results or recovery.
  Hover/focus, scroll position, passive data drift and transient obstruction do
  not become Variants.

  representative screenshot per Variant. The writer creates one schema-v3 State

  landing Variant States.

  runs until the existing Page Identity specialist compares the current

  accepts its natural-language evidence. Reusing a known Variant adds no
  specialist call.
- Pending Entry Review now persists the exact semantic Variant and source State.

  pending records recover only when the Page has exactly one State; an ambiguous
  multi-State record is discarded for fresh observation rather than attaching
  Entry evidence to the wrong State.

  reconstruction and Page Identity candidate projection. Separate regressions

### 2026-08-10 - Bind autonomous Region/Entry occurrences to exact Variant States

- Reaffirmed the runtime ownership boundary: the formal autonomous traverser is
  Qwen. New States use the model-neutral `perception_mode=autonomous_vlm`;
  `luna_autonomous` remains accepted only when reading old graph evidence. Luna
  is not a graph writer, runtime Agent or collection contract.
- Region frame state now records the exact current `state_id`; observation-local
  bbox data is cleared when either frame or State changes. Stable Region groups
  accumulate the `state_ids` of their real occurrences across material Variants,
  and merge/resume preserve that membership.
- Stable Entries now accumulate `source_state_ids` when the same identity is
  observed in multiple Variants. The first scalar source remains only for the
  source-local inferred-edge compatibility path; it is not used to copy an
  occurrence across States.
- The autonomous loop passes the registered scene State into Region/Entry writes.
  A material Variant with no accepted Region occurrence must return to
  `record_regions`. Inferred edges prefer explicit occurrence States and refuse
  ambiguous Page-only fallback instead of selecting the first Variant.
- The collection projection validates Region/Entry State membership and Page
  ownership, then writes each semantic block/Entry Element only to its declared
  States. Legacy sidecars without membership remain compatible only for a
  single-State Page; multi-State ambiguity, cross-Page references and missing
  owner Region occurrences fail closed.
- Writer schemas are now autonomous Regions v5, Region groups v2 and Entries v4.
  The Clock World regression uses the actual autonomous writer output to produce
  four material Variant States, persists exact occurrences, passes
  `StateGraph.load`, runs the formal projection and then resumes without losing
  the four Variant catalog. A separate regression blocks inferred edges when the
  source Page has multiple candidate Variant States.
- Verification:
  - Focused Qwen autonomous/occurrence/projection/resume/task/M13 suite:
    `269 passed in 8.44s`.

### 2026-08-10 - Let Qwen report all supported business-effect kinds

- Replaced the Qwen main-turn `previous_action.state_change` field with one
  `previous_action.business_effect` contract rather than adding parallel
  object/query fields. The report carries one of `state_change`,
  `object_creation` or `query_result`, an exact approved effect Region,
  before/after fact, user-facing capability name and concrete parameter
  bindings.
- Reused the existing before/after settlement and append-only
  `effect_observations[]` ledger. A supported record is written only for a
  formal Entry or Region probe whose intent matched, screenshot bytes changed,
  landing stayed on the same canonical Page and reported Region resolves to a
  committed formal Region. Navigation, landing and pixel change never imply a
  business effect. No Agent, Reviewer call, sidecar or graph schema was added.
- The old `state_change` response key now fails closed instead of being
  silently ignored. Removed the unproduced singular
  `attempt.evidence.effect_observation` induction fallback; every writer,
  StateGraph API and current document uses the canonical plural append-only
  field. A regression proves that the obsolete spelling cannot induce a
  capability.
- Updated the current traversal, capability-induction and global contracts.

  occurrences, cross-task recipe composition, cleanup induction and fresh-Qwen
  live reliability remain explicitly open.

### 2026-08-10 - Derive executable parameters from real Qwen action values

- Fixed capability grouping so repeated Qwen probes with different concrete
  inputs no longer become unrelated literal recipes. Before recipe comparison,

  distinct observed values and contains that slot. A Qwen binding that appears

### 2026-08-10 - Infer evidence-backed cleanup cycles without another executor

- Capability induction now emits a top-level `cleanup_cycle` only when an
  `effect_verified` object creation and removal have at least two inverse
  Region/fact witnesses. Every committed Attempt in each evidence episode must
  form one continuous `landing_verified` State path, and the cleanup path must
  reverse the source baseline/effect pair.
- Parameter links are accepted only when the cleanup parameter maps uniquely to
  a creation parameter and at least two shared concrete values proved that
  mapping. The relation stores capability/effect references rather than copying
  a second `cleanup_recipe` into the creation capability.
- Task synthesis accepts an exact `--relation-id` and turns the relation into
  two ordinary capability refs with fixed order and an explicit cleanup
  dependency. It reuses the existing M13 authority hydration, recipe binder and
  executor; no Qwen Prompt field, Agent call, sidecar, graph schema or cleanup
  executor was added.
- Updated the current capability-induction, collection and global contracts.

### 2026-08-10 - Compile observed Entry-to-effect recipes without a general DAG

- Capability induction now walks backward from a final Qwen-observed effect over
  the chronological ActionAttempt ledger and includes the connected formal Entry
  prefix only when every committed step is `landing_verified` and adjacent
  source/landing States match. An intermediate `query_result` may contribute
  parameters; an earlier stateful effect, a broken State chain or a parameter
  conflict stops composition. Without a safe explicit Entry, the original local
  ProbeEpisode remains unchanged.
- This compiles an actually observed `open entry -> type query -> select result`
  sequence into one executable object-effect recipe without adding a general
  planning DAG, inferred edge, model call, Prompt field, sidecar or schema.
- Confirmed multi-parameter recipes now retain complete observed
  `parameter_examples`. Task synthesis selects one real tuple instead of taking
  the first value from each independent domain; cleanup relation synthesis
  requires a jointly observed source/cleanup assignment. M13 rejects a tuple
  whose individual values are valid but were never observed together.
- Updated the current capability-induction, visual-collection and global
  contracts. The formal producer remains Qwen; `luna_autonomous` remains only a
  read-compatibility label for old graphs.

### 2026-08-10 - Give Qwen one reviewed Region-query input path

- Added one cross-platform autonomous tool,
  `input_text(target, point_1000, text)`. It is exposed only during
  `probe_region`, never during Page survey or Entry exploration; it has no
  `entry_id`, does not press Enter, and accepts only a short non-empty
  single-line value. No new Agent, service, controller, sidecar or graph schema
  was introduced.
- Reused the existing Click Reviewer for the combined focus-and-type action.
  It now receives the exact proposed text and must verify both that the marked
  point is the requested visible input and that typing itself is a
  non-sensitive, non-submitting search/filter/query operation. The runtime
  binds the reviewed field to its formal Region and framework-owned stable
  action Element UID, writes the ordinary before/after ActionAttempt, and sends
  desktop `TYPING(x,y,text)` or Android `input_text(x,y,text)`. The desktop
  adapter now focuses optional x/y before typing, matching the existing Android
  and Local HTML behavior.
- Kept the evidence pipeline direct: pending Qwen context retains the exact
  entered text for effect settlement; the existing business-effect contract
  can report `query_result` and parameter bindings; capability induction also
  parameterizes Android's top-level `text`. Reviewed input actions project as
  `el_type=input`; `projected_elements` now counts all distinct projected
  Element UIDs rather than only Entry IDs.
- Closed the corresponding replay gap without another protocol. M13 aliases
  `TYPING/input_text` to TYPE; when a semantic selector is present it resolves
  the stable UID (or label plus formal Region) on a fresh screenshot, verifies
  an input-like target, and emits `CLICK -> TYPE`. Selector-free legacy TYPE
  retains its current-focus behavior. Router native input actions likewise
  accept the stable Element UID and replace saved coordinates with a fresh
  center.
- Added focused contracts for tool visibility/validation, Reviewer payload,
  desktop and Android focus-before-type dispatch, Qwen Region-probe Attempt and
  query recipe evidence, input Element projection/top-level text
  parameterization, and M13 selector-based fresh grounding.
- Verification:
  - targeted `py_compile` for all changed runtime, environment, induction,
    collection, Engine and test files passed;
  - the focused pytest command could not start because the Codex execution
    approval service reported its usage limit. This is missing runtime test
    evidence, not a passing or failing test result; it must be rerun before this
    slice is accepted.
- No Qwen API call, live Clock traversal, emulator/VM GUI action, M13 episode or
  full framework regression/certification gate was run. The implementation does
  not claim full visible Element inventory, result/created-object identity,

## 2026-08-15 - Stop homogeneous Region-probe value enumeration

- Separated traversal coverage from capability evidence certification. One
  settled representative result now covers that semantic operation type for

  Open Regions with no supported capability are scheduled before Regions that
  already have representative evidence.

  the latest screenshot and decides whether another distinct safe visible core
  operation remains. `complete_region_probe` now explicitly permits completion
  after one representative success when it accounts for the only remaining
  operation, while another distinct visible control still keeps the Region open.

  settled Region-probe `input_text` records `input_text:<reviewed Element UID>`
  in probe progress. A later input on that same reviewed Element is rejected as

## 2026-08-15 - Keep names stable during Region review corrections

  Entry target and owner that was not challenged and still agrees with the
  latest screenshot. Correcting bbox, coverage, summary or another local field
  must not silently rename or rebind an identity. A rename/rebind is allowed
  only when the latest screenshot proves the old identity or owner wrong, or
  the Reviewer explicitly requires the identity/partition change; the Agent

## 2026-08-15 - Remove Region bbox from semantic registration

- Removed `bbox_1000` from the model-facing Region schema and Region ledger
  projection, rather than leaving it as an optional field. New and existing
  Regions are registered from their stable functional name, summary, entries
  and semantic owner without spending VLM context on current-frame geometry.
  Legacy checkpoint or internal State-local geometry remains readable but is
  not exposed to the main Agent or Reviewer.

  Region because its optional rectangle touches OS chrome. It still rejects
  proposals that semantically register the desktop, Dock, another application
  or window-management controls as target-application functions, and it still
  owns Region grouping, active-surface and cross-Page equivalence decisions.

  explicit assigned Region and the latest screenshot instead of failing the
  point-in-bbox gate. Existing geometric restriction remains when a current
  bbox is available.

  Entry ownership were clear but Region Review repeatedly moved pixel
  boundaries around the left Dock and right window controls. Per the user's
  instruction, no offline or static test command was run before starting the

## 2026-08-15 - Stage Page identity before Variant identity

- Replaced the combined autonomous landing identity with two strict model
  rounds. `identify_page` exposes and accepts only Page `name/identity`; it
  contains no Variant field, candidate, current value or pending-action source
  Variant. After the Page is held by the framework, `identify_variant` exposes
  only that Page's material Variants; a new Page receives an empty candidate
  list. The two short phase contracts explain this flow without adding an
  application-, Page- or incident-specific rule.
- Enforced the boundary in runtime as well as the Qwen/Codex schemas. A custom
  or legacy Agent cannot commit a Page and injected Variant in one turn. A
  restored map with no current Page clears any stale current Variant and
  re-enters Page identity. Page metadata returned by the specialist is retained
  through Variant commit; a known Page keeps its registered summary and surface
  kind instead of being overwritten by the landing name.
- Added an explicit Variant-to-Page correction transition. When the Agent
  disputes Page ownership during Variant review, the staged owner and stale
  Variant proposal are cleared, Page-only review restarts without seeding the
  disputed name as a proposed new Page, and evidence distinguishes the disputed
  staged Page from the trusted action-source Page.
- Allowed reviewed interruption recovery while Page/Variant identity is still
  unresolved. The obstructed frame is not registered; the original landing
  action remains pending. When re-observation changes the frame, wait or a
  successful hover/click/back clears the old identity proposal, staged Page,
  current Page/Variant binding, feedback and frozen landing assessment, then
  re-runs Page -> Variant on the fresh screenshot. Recovery provenance is
  explicit in trace/history with `semantic_graph_recorded=false`. The hard
  `max_actions` bound cannot be exceeded, but pure identity settlement after
  the last permitted recovery action is not truncated.
- The motivating Clock run stopped on Timer because one combined turn mixed
  Timer Page judgment with Alarms Variant candidates and repeatedly produced a
  rejected Page/Variant pair. This change fixes the general ownership/data-flow
  boundary; it does not encode Clock, Timer or a preferred Variant name.
- Verification (local offline):
  `python -m pytest tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_completion_report.py tests/test_autonomous_entry_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py -q -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp <system-temp>`
  -> `304 passed in 9.60s`. `py_compile` passed for all 18 changed autonomous
  runtime Python files, and scoped `git diff --check` passed.
- No new Qwen API call, GUI/VM action, fresh Clock traversal, full framework
  regression gate or completion/certification run was performed. The result is
  offline contract verification, not live traversal acceptance.

## 2026-08-16 - Give Qwen specific safe contract feedback and close unassessed actions

- Audited the model-visible rejection paths before changing code. Page,

  boundary, where JSON, parser, phase and tool-contract failures were collapsed
  into one generic correction. No per-Reviewer rewrite or application-specific
  Prompt rule was added.

  flows. Invalid JSON or a non-object root, plus the controlled errors returned
  by `parse_turn`, now reach the second Qwen attempt and the same-task outer
  correction as a concrete field/stage reason. Provider and transport details
  remain debug-only and receive a fixed safe retry. The classification is
  carried as state rather than inferred from an error-string prefix, and the
  implementation directly reuses the parser message instead of maintaining a
  field-by-field Prompt mapping table. The Codex adapter was not modified; its
  legacy correction behavior remains the default shared-helper path.

  assessment never becomes valid. Five rejected assessment turns no longer
  suspend that task and schedule unrelated work while the old action remains
  pending. The run stops with `pending_action_assessment_limit`; the action is
  retained as executed but `assessment_unavailable`, its landing is neither
  verified nor committed, an explicitly bound Entry becomes
  unresolved/non-eligible, ledger pending is cleared, and completion retains a
  gap. This prevents both false success and duplicate execution after resume.
- Added focused regressions for field-level retry feedback, malformed JSON,
  array/null roots, raw-response isolation, backend prefix spoofing, outer
  feedback preservation, and the pending-action task boundary.

## 2026-08-16 - Reject Region completion from the wrong material Variant

  framework routed a Region probe to the correct Page but not necessarily to

  therefore closed `Alarm Configuration Fields` because the Region was absent,

  was a false completion, not evidence that the Region had been inspected.

## 2026-08-17 - Split autonomous Prompt roles and make traversal framework-owned

- Reworked autonomous task selection as one cross-category pool. When a task
  must be selected, distance is the shortest edge count in the committed,
  landing-verified StateGraph route view; equal distances use persisted task
  creation order. Survey, Entry and Region work has no fixed class priority.
- A selected exact Entry remains current through verified-route navigation,
  scrolling, locating and recovery, including intermediate landings on another
  Page. The global pool is recomputed only after that Entry settles, establishes
  a prerequisite dependency, or is explicitly suspended/terminal. Region
  operations remain in one `Region@Page@state` task and a qualifying
  `action=null` closes it.
- Targets without a verified route are not dispatched for guessed navigation.
  They stop as `unverified_route_remaining` and completion records the exact
  missing-route gap. Rejection-suspended tasks also enter completion gaps.
- Split the Qwen main call into a stable Chinese system prefix plus a dynamic
  user message. The system message carries role/safety, active tool meanings
  and the strict schema; the user message carries current task/stage, screenshot
  order, minimal ledger context, corrections and the complete chronological
  current-task history. API cache-token details are retained in debug records.
- Reduced `page_update` to
  `regions[{name,summary,coverage_complete,entries[{target}]}]`. GUI actions
  require one purpose and cannot share a turn with `page_update`; Android
  scrolling remains separate from double-tap and long-press gestures.
- Split Page and Page-state-version identity. Region partition and Entry review
  remain separate stateless roles. Proposed-action and interruption review use
  one two-mode Action Reviewer, while one batch before/after Region mapper
  validates a complete unique assignment. Only exact one-to-one Region
  continuity is committed automatically; grouped matches remain audit evidence.
- Region internal button discovery and execution now share one
  `explore_region` task. Per-button, Region-complete and run-finish tools are not
  model-visible; the framework derives task and run completion from the ledger,
  pending settlement and restoration state.
- Fixed premature completion after GUI dispatch: an Entry becoming `attempted`
  no longer lets task synchronization finish the run before its Page/Variant
  identity and real action result have settled.
- Verification (local offline):
  `C:\\Users\\Admin\\miniconda3\\envs\\guiwalk\\python.exe -m pytest tests/test_autonomous_action_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_completion_report.py tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py tests/test_vlm_timeout.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py -q --tb=short -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp=.pytest-autonomous-suite-0817`
  -> `251 passed in 11.40s`.
  `C:\\Users\\Admin\\miniconda3\\envs\\guiwalk\\python.exe -m py_compile <all changed Python paths>`
  -> passed.
- No Qwen API call, emulator/device traversal, deploy-server run, full framework
  regression, release or certification gate was run.

## 2026-08-17 - Defer a visibly blocked Entry to one concrete prerequisite

- Added the minimal `defer_current_task(prerequisite_target, reason)` main-Agent
  tool. It is available only for an `explore_entry` task and requires the just
  settled exact attempt to be `no_visible_change`, mismatched to the requested
  result, and still unclassified. The prerequisite must uniquely match an open
  formal Entry; empty, self and cyclic links are rejected.
- Deferred work is persisted with its reason, omitted from nearest-task
  selection, and automatically re-enters the pool after its prerequisite has a
  verified/inferred result. A skipped or blocked prerequisite produces a named
  completion gap. A correct, interactive exact action with no visible change
  now adds an `explored_no_visible_effect` history record instead of inventing
  a prerequisite.
- Focused local offline verification:
  `C:\\Users\\Admin\\miniconda3\\envs\\guiwalk\\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\\Users\\Admin\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\guiwalk-task-scheduler-elevated-20260817 tests/test_autonomous_task_scheduler.py`
  -> `4 passed in 0.64s`.
  Targeted `py_compile` over all changed runtime modules and this test -> passed.
- No Qwen API call, emulator/device traversal, deploy-server run, full framework
  regression, release or certification gate was run.

## 2026-08-17 - Make Region survey the hard parent gate for Entry tasks

- Replaced new-run scheduling of free `explore_region` internal-operation work
  with a strict Page/Region occurrence ownership boundary. `survey_page` now
  owns observation, local scrolling, Entry cataloguing and review only; every
  reviewed Entry remains one durable child task through routing, locating,
  scrolling, waiting, action and settlement.
- Entry candidates now include both navigation/function-surface controls and
  safe Region-local core operations such as start/pause/toggles and query
  inputs. They use the same exact Entry ledger and `explore_entry` lifecycle;
  only an exact `control_type=input` Entry receives `input_text` in its compact
  tool view.
- An Entry whose owner Region lacks `coverage_complete` or an accepted Entry
  audit remains recorded but cannot enter the executable pool. At equal verified
  graph distance, an incomplete Region survey on the current Page precedes a
  ready Entry; remote surveys remain ordinary route candidates and do not form
  a global barrier. A held exact Entry still retains priority until it settles,
  defers or terminates.
- Region exploration closure is framework-derived: a completed survey with all
  child Entries terminal is `complete` only when they are verified/inferred;
  another terminal child outcome yields `partial`. Historical Region probe
  progress remains evidence/repeat-action data but no longer schedules a
  multi-operation Agent task.
- Focused local offline verification:

## 2026-08-17 - Recheck a target after its prerequisite is actually handled

- Changed the minimal deferred-Entry state machine so a prerequisite settling is
  not treated as proof that its dependent was unlocked. A verified or unexpected
  real action, or a real terminal no-visible-change probe, moves the dependent to
  `recheck_after_prerequisite`; that exact target resumes ahead of ordinary ready
  work through a verified route, and its compact Prompt asks the same Qwen main
  Agent to use the latest screenshot to re-locate and re-check it. A one-shot
  release marker prevents an unresolved prerequisite from repeatedly preempting
  later route, scroll or locate steps.
- Explicit inferred equivalence remains semantic reuse only and cannot supply a
  current-State prerequisite effect. Missing, unsafe, unreachable and other
  terminal prerequisites produce one root gap plus traceable affected-task
  gaps. A rechecked target may name a different exact prerequisite, but the
  same handled prerequisite is rejected to prevent an `E -> P -> E` loop.
  The compact prerequisite result and recheck facts now persist in the Region
  checkpoint; restore preserves them without guessing that the target is ready.
- Focused local offline verification:
  `C:\Users\Admin\miniconda3\envs\guiwalk\python.exe -m pytest -q --tb=short -p no:cacheprovider --basetemp C:\Users\Admin\AppData\Local\Temp\gui_rewalk_prereq_root_20260817_1 tests/test_autonomous_task_scheduler.py`
  -> `13 passed in 0.95s`.
  Adjacent main-loop/tool/completion verification with a fresh system-temp
  `--basetemp` over `tests/test_autonomous_task_scheduler.py`,
  `tests/test_autonomous_loop.py`, `tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py`,
  and `tests/test_autonomous_completion_report.py` -> `230 passed in 9.74s`.
  `C:\Users\Admin\miniconda3\envs\guiwalk\python.exe -m py_compile gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_scheduling.py gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_runtime.py gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_context.py gui_rewalk/src/core/visual_traversal/runtime/autonomous_completion.py tests/test_autonomous_task_scheduler.py`

## 2026-08-17 - Version shared-Region coverage and compile partial bundles

- Added evidence-bearing shared-Region coverage versions without changing the
  Qwen page-update schema. An accepted Entry Review publishes the normalized
  `control_type + target` signature. Only an exact one-to-one before/after batch
  Region Mapping with the same signature may inherit that survey/audit result;
  name, position, layout similarity or an ordinary cross-Page semantic binding
  cannot. A new or missing core Entry stays on the normal local review path and
  publishes another version after acceptance.
- Bumped `autonomous_regions.json` to v6 and its Region-group sidecar to v3;
  occurrences now retain coverage version and basis, with backward-tolerant
  restore. Inherited Regions still commit their visible Entries and obey the
  existing explicit verified-result reuse contract; inheritance does not mark
  an Entry result verified.
- Added framework-derived Page -> Region -> Entry complete/open/gap counts.
  `autonomous_completion.json` v3 now separates `traversal_status` from
  `bundle_status`. The CLI attempts evidence-bundle compilation for partial runs
  too, and a compile failure no longer overwrites factual traversal gaps.
- Focused local offline verification with fresh system-temporary pytest roots:
  the Region-version/mapping cases -> `3 passed, 180 deselected in 1.12s`;
  `tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py tests/test_autonomous_loop.py` ->

  `tests/test_capability_induction.py` -> `11 passed in 0.38s`.
- No Qwen API call, emulator/device traversal, deploy-server run, full framework

## 2026-08-17 - Stop repeating fixed identity and route Entries by State

- A real isolated desktop Clocks run reached the visible World Clock empty
  state, but a corrected Region proposal repeatedly renamed the already
  reviewed Variant and stopped at `all_remaining_work_suspended`. A real
  isolated Android Clock run later repeated the same no-action identity drift
  and stopped at `framework_rejection_limit`.
- Ordinary main-Agent stages with no pending GUI action now omit `screen` from
  the strict response schema and bind the current reviewed Page/Variant in the
  parser. Identity stages retain their separate minimal schemas, and action
  settlement still reports the real landing identity. The main Agent can still
  request `page_identity` or `report_record_error` when the latest screenshot
  conflicts with the ledger.
- The Android run also showed a verified Entry action sourced from a new State
  on the same Page while the Entry and Region occurrence ledgers named only
  earlier States; partial-bundle compilation correctly failed closed. Entry
  scheduling now targets recorded source State IDs. A retained same-Page Entry
  uses a verified State route when available; otherwise the unrecorded current
  Variant survey runs first and records the occurrence before the Entry action.
- Initial focused regression after this change: the two new Prompt/parse cases
  -> `2 passed, 170 deselected`; the State-route case plus adjacent cases ->
  `4 passed, 183 deselected`; and the first affected Tier 3 suite ->
  `252 passed, 3 failed`. The three failures were fixtures with no registered
  graph State but arbitrary `source_state_id` strings; preserving the existing
  Page fallback only when the runtime has no current State made those four
  focused cases pass. Final affected Tier 3 rerun over
  `tests/test_autonomous_protocol.py`, `tests/test_autonomous_task_scheduler.py`,
  `tests/test_autonomous_loop.py` and
  `tests/test_autonomous_tool_integration.py` -> `255 passed in 9.57s`.
  The directly adjacent capability compiler/completion-report suite ->
  `14 passed in 0.40s`; targeted `py_compile` and `git diff --check` passed.
- The two live runs above validate the failure reproduction only; they do not
  validate this fix. No release or full framework regression gate was run.

## 2026-08-17 - Ignore out-of-scope conservative Region deferrals

- The isolated Android Clock rerun reached an Alarm time-selection dialog and
  correctly kept its occluded Alarm-list Regions incomplete. The stateless
  Entry Reviewer nevertheless repeated those two formal Region names in
  `deferred_regions` although the focused request exposed only three different
  `coverage_audit_regions`; five identical invalid replies suspended the survey.

  recorded as `entry_review_scope_normalized` and ignored as a no-op: they
  cannot change formal coverage facts, while already-incomplete occluded
  Regions remain queued for a later unobstructed survey.

  tool-integration suite -> `255 passed in 9.52s`; targeted `py_compile` and

  above reproduces the failure and does not validate this fix.

## 2026-08-17 - Allow prerequisite deferral before a blocked Entry attempt

- The next isolated Android Clock run opened a time-picker dialog. The main
  Agent correctly reported that the modal blocked the assigned bottom-nav
  Entry, but runtime demanded a no-visible-change click on that blocked target
  before accepting `defer_current_task`; five repeated rejections suspended the
  Entry. This contradicted the intended visible-prerequisite workflow.
- An `explore_entry` may now defer either before its first exact attempt when the
  latest screenshot shows a blocker, or after a no-visible-change exact attempt.
  The prerequisite still must bind to exactly one open formal Entry; a dialog or
  Region name is not accepted in place of its concrete control. The compact tool
  description now says this explicitly.
- The focused scheduler suite -> `16 passed in 1.00s`; the affected Tier 3
  autonomous protocol/scheduler/loop/tool-integration suite -> `256 passed in
  9.51s`. This is offline validation; the live run above reproduces the failure
  and does not validate this fix.

## 2026-08-17 - Separate Region identity continuity from visible State routing

- A fresh latest-code Android Clock run on the isolated `emulator-5652` reached
  the time picker keyboard Variant after nine real action edges. The main Agent
  and independent reviewers correctly excluded the hidden clock dial from the
  current visible partition, but the Region occurrence still contained the
  current State in its identity-continuity `state_ids`. The scheduler therefore
  reopened the same survey: trace history grew from 255 to 297 while action
  edges stayed at nine. The owned run was stopped with TERM and recorded exit
  143; this reproduces the remaining boundary and is not a successful traversal
  or bundle result.
- Region group schema v4 now separates `state_ids` from
  `visible_state_ids`. Only a consistent independent Entry coverage audit (or
  exact inherited coverage audit) adds the current State to
  `visible_state_ids`; identity binding and batch continuity cannot. Incomplete
  Region routing prefers these reviewed visible States. Legacy snapshots fall
  back to exact coverage-version source States, then to the old occurrence only
  when neither reviewed source exists.
- The focused scheduler and Region-registry suites -> `30 passed in 1.11s`.
  The affected Tier 3 autonomous protocol/scheduler/loop/tool-integration plus
  Region-registry suite -> `270 passed in 9.57s`; targeted `py_compile` and
  `git diff --check` passed.
- A post-fix fresh Android run used commit `9afd046b`, isolated
  `emulator-5652` and run root
  `android_clock_visible_region_v4_20260817_0540`. It naturally stopped at the
  internal ten-action budget with six States, nine graph edges and five of
  eighteen Entries verified. The completion report remained incomplete with
  nineteen concrete gaps, while bundle compilation passed with four
  capabilities and eighteen projected Elements. This validates the latest
  source on a real device and the partial-bundle path, but the stochastic run
  followed Alarm to Clock and did not revisit the exact keyboard-hidden dial;
  the precise visibility-routing fix therefore remains supported by the live
  failure reproduction plus the focused offline regression, not a matching
  post-fix live path.

## 2026-08-17 - Normalize repeated existing Entries outside focused review

  now settles once as unresolved `observed_no_visible_change` even when the
  main Agent leaves `failure_kind=null`. A changed frame still requires normal
  settlement, so closing a foreground surface cannot be hidden as no effect.
  Suspending one bound task after five rejected turns now clears the cross-task
  streak and returns control to the global scheduler. The ten-turn run guard is
  retained only for rejection loops with no bound task identity. No Prompt,
  Agent role, application rule or new model field was added.
- Focused no-effect, changed-frame and cross-task regressions -> `3 passed in
  0.90s`. Directly affected loop/tool-integration/task-scheduler suites -> `243
  passed in 10.02s`; targeted `py_compile` and `git diff --check` passed.
  Post-fix live mobile validation is pending.

## 2026-08-17 - Normalize a unique Variant name used as a Page name

- The isolated `a1ce2e7a` Android Clock rerun used `emulator-5582`, ports
  5582/5583/8582 and run root
  `android_clock_no_effect_postfix_20260817_0945`. It performed eight real GUI
  actions and recorded five States. Qwen correctly observed that the 8:30 alarm
  enable switch changed from blue/on to gray/off, but during the Page-only
  landing step repeatedly reported the registered Variant name
  `Alarm - Expanded Card` as the Page. Runtime rejected that name because the
  registered Page is `Alarm`, then stopped at
  `pending_action_assessment_limit`. This is live failure reproduction, not fix
  validation; the partial bundle still compiled with twenty-five projected
  Elements, three capabilities and zero relations. The run did not reach the
  exact Timer no-effect branch. Its device, process and assigned ports were
  released.
- During a Page-only landing step, a `known` name that is not a Page but
  uniquely matches one registered Variant is now normalized to that Variant's
  owning Page. The following Variant stage still performs ordinary material
  state classification. If the same Variant name belongs to multiple Pages,
  runtime keeps the result ambiguous and rejects it rather than guessing. The
  normalization is deterministic and adds no Prompt, Agent role, application
  rule or model field.
- Focused unique-owner, ambiguous-owner and full pending-action settlement
  regressions -> `2 passed in 1.19s`. Directly affected
  loop/tool-integration/task-scheduler suites -> `245 passed in 10.23s`;
  targeted `py_compile` and `git diff --check` passed.
- A fresh `205ad5f2` Android Clock run used isolated `emulator-5584`, ports
  5584/5585/8584 and run root
  `android_clock_variant_alias_postfix_20260817_0955`. It naturally stopped
  `framework_partial` after eight real actions and six States, with no task
  suspension, `framework_rejection_limit` or
  `pending_action_assessment_limit`. Its partial bundle compiled with
  thirty-nine projected Elements, six capabilities and zero relations; eight
  Entries were complete and thirty-one remained open across two Pages. The
  stochastic path did not reproduce either the unique Variant-as-Page alias or
  the exact Timer no-effect branch, so those exact fixes remain certified by
  focused offline regressions rather than claimed as live path acceptance.
  The emulator, run process and all three assigned ports were released. Across
  eighty-two Qwen calls, reported `cached_tokens` remained zero; main-Agent
  prompt input ranged from 3,004 to 13,871 tokens, so cache behavior remains a
  separate performance gap.

## 2026-08-17 - Keep unresolved Entry coverage runnable

- The final Android Clock run stopped `framework_partial` while the current
  keyboard frame still had incomplete visible Regions. The scheduler exposed
  only the first incomplete Region on a Page; that earlier clock-dial Region had
  no verified reverse route from the keyboard State and masked the visible
  keyboard and bottom-control surveys.
- Page survey scheduling now evaluates every incomplete Region independently.
  An unreachable hidden Region remains a concrete gap but cannot suppress a
  visible sibling Region from the global task pool.
- Exact formal `(Region,target,control_type)` Entries repeated in a new
  `page_update` are removed before Entry Review. The Reviewer still audits the
  requested Region for missing targets, while an already-known Entry can no
  longer create a false disagreement merely because the Reviewer omits it.
  Exact known Entries returned by the Reviewer as new findings are likewise
  normalized as no-ops.
- Entry Reviewer records are bound to the survey task that requested the review
  before the scheduler chooses subsequent work. This keeps the full verdict in
  the correct task history instead of attaching it to a newly selected Entry.

## 2026-08-17 - Add occurrence-local Region state for repeated controls

- A reviewed `page_update` can keep repeated, independently stateful components
  as separate Region occurrences while grouping later instances with
  `same_group_as`. The Region registry now persists stable occurrence refs,
  reusable Region state definitions, verified transitions and a sparse mapping
  from Page State to each occurrence state. No screenshot hash is used.
- Entry records now persist their owner Region, representative occurrence and
  allowed local states. Scheduling uses those local states before the legacy
  whole-Page `source_state_ids`, so a change inside one alarm item does not make
  unchanged navigation or sibling controls appear remote.

  `owner_structure` result records the natural-language reason as the local
  transition effect, preserves every unaffected occurrence state and reopens
  only the owner Region for Entry review. Page-scoped or uncertain changes keep
  the previous whole-Page resurvey path. No Agent role or specialist call was
  added, and the static Prompt gained only the general repeated-instance rule.

  exit code 0. Focused new contracts -> `6 passed in 0.88s`. Region, Entry and
  scheduler module suites plus directly adjacent Prompt, commit and same-Page
  settlement contracts -> `58 passed in 1.65s`; two sidecar persistence and
  minimal response-schema contracts -> `2 passed in 1.36s`. `git diff --check`
  passed. The
  first sandboxed pytest basetemp had Windows ACL errors after two tests; the
  successful results above used an independent basetemp outside the sandbox.
  No Android emulator, desktop VM or live Qwen traversal was run in this change.

  看不到的对象不能被改写成当前可见事实。入口复核器若以遮挡、裁切或模糊解释原候选，必须把该候选写入结构化延期，
  不能一边说延期一边从清单删除。延期区域和候选保持待办，取得新截图后再复核，不能用 `omitted_regions` 删除。

- 第二次隔离桌面 Clock 运行最后打开了贪睡时长菜单。该入口真实生效，但浮层遮挡时间设置区域；入口复核器要求在无遮挡
  状态重查后，调度器发现当前 State 到目标区域唯一已登记可见 State 没有验证路线，便在再次调用主 Agent 前过滤了任务，
  最终以 `framework_partial` 停止。此前关闭响铃时长菜单的动作能够成功，是因为下一个精确入口仍在执行，主 Agent 将其作为
  中间恢复动作；最后一个入口完成后没有相同机会。
- 同一 Page 内的目标 Region 或 Entry 只在其他已登记 State 可用、且图中暂无转换时，任务现在仍保持可运行。任务上下文明确
  当前 Page 已符合但目标条件未满足、暂无已验证状态转换；主 Agent 根据最新截图逐步选择安全的局部导航或恢复动作。每个真实
  步骤继续走现有点击复核、前后图结算和动作边写入，落到任一满足目标条件的 State 后继续原任务，不要求回到最初 State。
  跨 Page 的未知路线仍不开放，已有路线和当前可见兄弟区域仍优先。
- 任务还会向主 Agent 显式投影一条精简的目标条件：区域任务要求该区域重新完整可见、可调查，入口任务要求指定操作在所属区域中
  重新可见、可定位。它不携带旧坐标，也不限定唯一原始状态。

  `framework_partial` 结束，因此 Qwen 实际未被调用。当前主循环先让未绑定的 live 帧完成 Page/Variant 身份，再计算任务和完成缺口。

## 2026-08-18 - 区域复核不把应用外临时浮层算入结构

- `d9059916` 的独立 Clocks 实跑在首个页面被区域复核三轮耗尽：系统更新通知一轮被要求纳入内容区、下一轮又被要求排除，顶部应用栏也在合并与拆分之间反复。主 Agent 最后已经排除通知并重报稳定区域，但任务仍以 `all_remaining_work_suspended` 停止，因此该轮没有到达新建闹钟页，也不能作为参数任务筛选的验收。
- 区域复核提示沿用原有目标应用边界并收紧裁决：系统通知、桌面或其他应用浮层、窗口管理控件不属于目标应用 Region，提案排除它们不算遗漏，也不能用它们改变应用区域边界；临时浮层遮住区域关系时返回不确定，不猜测被遮住结构。没有新增模型角色、输出字段或应用专用规则。
- 聚焦验证 `tests/test_autonomous_region_tools.py` 为 `14 passed in 0.58s`；修改运行模块的 `py_compile` 与 `git diff --check` 通过。未执行全框架回归。
- 随后把提交 `5424426d` 部署到 js1 的独立 Docker run root `desktop_clocks_non_tasks_5424426d_20260818_220922`，使用 `qwen3.7-plus` 和 20 个动作上限。运行建立了 World 空状态页和 Add New World Clock 对话框，真实点击左上角 `+`；入口复核器也真实输出了 `non_task_entries`，把当前禁用的 Add 归为非任务，同时保留会改变搜索结果区域的输入框。主 Agent 认为 Add 是等待输入前置条件的确认操作而拒绝永久丢弃，三轮后形成最终分歧；首屏四个可见导航标签也在区域修订时被漏掉，所以未到达 Alarms。运行以 `framework_partial`、9 个 gap、退出码 3 收尾；部分证据 bundle 编译成功，本轮独立容器已释放。这证明显式三分结构已经接入真实调用，但也暴露出“暂时不可用的功能操作应延期而非归入非任务”和“区域修订不能丢失先前清晰帧中的可见导航”两个未完成边界，不能作为完整遍历验收。

## 2026-08-18 - 延期有价值操作并固定区域讨论证据

- 入口复核的三分类保持不变，但边界更精确：已经看清且没有后续探索价值的候选才进入 `non_task_entries`；功能有探索价值、只是因当前可见状态或前置条件不可执行的候选进入 `deferred_entries`。若主 Agent 对功能语义判断正确、复核器只调整执行时机，二者不算功能分歧。
- 延期 Region 在同一证据代不再反复派发。只有一次已验证且确实改变界面的动作产生新证据代后，调度器才重新检查；后台刷新、通知和其他被动像素变化不会解锁延期。若前置始终没有满足，任务保持 gap，不伪造完成。
- 一轮区域复核固定使用首次提案的完整截图。后续主 Agent 修订会同时获得该固定证据，区域复核器也继续查看同一图；接受、终局拒绝或真实界面变化后释放缓存。恢复运行时若内存中的固定图不可用，则从当前完整截图开始一轮新讨论，不把旧图写入 checkpoint。
- 没有新增模型角色、响应字段、应用或控件专用规则。聚焦验证为：区域/协议合同 `36 passed in 0.61s`，固定区域证据、终局重开、延期调度和入口结构四条运行回归 `4 passed in 1.37s`。相关运行模块 `py_compile` 与 `git diff --check` 通过；未执行全框架回归。本条记录时尚未执行新的真实桌面遍历。
- 随后将提交 `bae170e1` 部署到 js1 独立运行根 `desktop_clocks_fixed_review_bae170e1_20260818_2307`，使用新建 Docker、`qwen3.7-plus` 和 20 动作上限。固定区域证据真实生效：首轮提案中的四个导航标签在第二轮修订中全部保留，区域复核器使用同一固定截图接受合并后的顶部栏。
- 实跑也发现两个未解决的通用边界。入口复核器把当前可执行但“优先级较低”的四个导航标签误放入延期，说明延期仍可能被模型误用为调度优先级；添加世界时钟对话框的操作栏被要求合并后，主 Agent 和入口复核器没有把 Cancel/Add 重新归属到保留区域，因此未实际走到“禁用 Add 延期”的目标场景。输入 London 和结果列表变化被真实验证，但新状态出现后没有可运行的返回路线，运行以 `unverified_route_remaining`、5 个 gap、退出码 3 结束；部分证据 bundle 编译成功，新建容器已释放。这是部分运行证据，不是完整遍历验收。

## 2026-08-19 - 新页面通过后批量复核跨页 Region 身份

- 新页面的页面内 Region 划分先沿用现有 Reviewer 完成；写账前，尚未绑定身份的新 Region 再与
  Region Registry 中每个 canonical Region 的一个代表 occurrence 做一次批量跨页身份复核。请求只含
  临时 occurrence 编号、Region 名称/摘要、所属 Page 和对应完整截图，不包含 Entry 或按钮清单。
- Reviewer 只在稳定功能、所拥有的交互语义和复用含义一致时建立共享；每个输入 occurrence 必须恰好
  出现一次。框架拒绝遗漏、重复、未知引用，以及不是“一条当前 occurrence 对一条既有代表”的共享组。
  证据不足、调用异常或结构无效时，新 Region 保持独立。
- 合法共享只让当前 occurrence 复用既有 `region_ref` 并保留各 Page/State occurrence；这条路径不继承
  coverage、不迁移 Entry，也不改变任何 Entry 状态。原动作前后 Region mapper 仍先按原合同执行，
  跨页复核只处理它尚未绑定的新 Region，因此无效跨页结果不会抑制或覆盖原 mapping。
- `autonomous_region_groups.v6` 只在 occurrence 中新增首次绑定时的 `representative_variant_name`；截图仍
  复用自然地图已有 Page@Variant 代表图，不写入 Region JSON。跨页候选必须取得该精确 Variant 截图，
  找不到时跳过，不能退化为同一 Page 的第一张截图。

  occurrence 完整性、精确 Variant 截图、Entry 状态不继承、旧 mapper 独立执行和事务回退共

## 2026-08-20 - 入口发现帧按需复核与幻觉候选终止

- 收紧入口分类而不增加应用专用规则：普通参数、待提交表单字段、危险、不可逆或有外部效果但命令相关的操作进入
  `record_only_entries`；记录不授权执行。`non_task_entries` 只表示静态、装饰、应用外或没有命令语义。入口复核稳定提示
  仍低于原 1200 字符门限。
- 每批入口首次通过审核时保存一张不可变完整发现帧。只有精确 `explore_entry` 回到已接受来源 State，主 Agent 扫描 owner
  Region 后以 `problem_type=target_not_found` 报告定位失败，才调用同一 Entry 复核角色比较发现帧与当前帧；没有为所有图标
  增加预先悬停或额外调用。
- 只有“主 Agent 在来源 State 定位失败 + 独立双图复核判定发现帧不支持且当前帧缺失”同时成立时，Entry 才进入
  `invalidated` 终态。原 Page、Region、entry_id、截图和理由保留作审计，但该项不再派发、不阻塞完成，也不进入能力目录；
  其他双图结果只返回重试、恢复/延期或未解决反馈。
- `autonomous_entries.json` 升为 v8，旧快照缺少发现帧时保持原 Entry，不做猜测性撤销。聚焦验证覆盖提示长度和分类、账本
  恢复、固定发现帧写入、双图撤销、原语义争议、完成统计及能力目录排除，结果为 `53 passed in 0.74s`。本条记录时尚未
  运行全框架回归或真实桌面/移动端遍历；后续 live 结果单独补充。

  模型调用或应用专用规则。聚焦验证覆盖真实适配路径和相邻直接写账合同，结果为 `2 passed in 1.26s`；修改运行模块与
  测试模块 `py_compile`、`git diff --check` 通过。首次测试命令因系统 pytest 临时目录权限错误未进入测试体，改用本任务
  独立工作区临时目录后通过。修正后的真实桌面遍历结果另行记录。
- 修正提交 `38deeb83` 的独立 js1 桌面 Clock 运行根为
  `desktop_clocks_lazy_entry_38deeb83_20260820_1706`。运行执行 3 个真实点击和 28 次 Qwen 调用：Add World Clock 两次精确
  点击均无可见效果；主 Agent 两次报告定位异常时，按需双图复核均判定发现帧支持且当前仍可见，因此入口没有被错误撤销，
  第二次失败后以负面探索证据终止重试。菜单点击成功并登记同页菜单展开状态。该结果证明按需存在性复核进入了真实主链。

  也记录了顶部导航，但主 Agent 只提出空状态内容和顶部工具栏两个 Region，Region Reviewer 又错误称该划分完整；最终只登记
  Add World Clock 与菜单两个 Entry，却以 `framework_complete`、0 gap、退出码 0 结束。bundle 虽编译成功，但只有 2 个
